- [Evaluating and Benchmarking the System One Model Jev](https://github.com/AppliedMachineLearning-Lab/jev-benchmarking) - Code for the arXiv paper by Deußer, Sparrenberg and Sifa (arXiv:2609.37647, 2026) benchmarking TypeSafe Jev (pinned `jev-1.13.0`) zero-shot on 37 public datasets against two open-weight LLMs scored on identical requests; raw responses are on Zenodo so every number can be recomputed offline. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-benchmarking.md).
- [evidence-referee](https://github.com/ismaildasci/evidence-referee) - Claude Code plugin that checks "done" claims and small judgment calls with TypeSafe Jev and keeps receipts (distinct from claude-referee). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/evidence-referee.md).
- [ex_typesafe_ai](https://github.com/iamtalha-arshad/ex_typesafe_ai) - Unofficial Elixir client for the TypeSafe AI API — typed structs, Req-based HTTP with retries, and ergonomic noul/choice/score questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/iamtalha-arshad-ex-typesafe-ai.md).
- [ExcelPilot](https://github.com/vikramlingam/excelpilot) - Live Excel agent with Qwen planning and TypeSafe Jev for intent routing, tool gating, and claim checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/excelpilot.md).
- [fast-browser](https://github.com/hamza-sahin/fast-browser) - Playwright browser automation for Codex/MCP with TypeSafe Jev (or Laya) decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-browser.md).
- [fastcampus-jev](https://github.com/teddylee777/fastcampus-jev) - Korean Fastcampus Jev tutorial: 13 notebooks + React/LangGraph demo (tool Choice, guardrails, risk gates, RAG) via OpenRouter (MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fastcampus-jev.md).
- [fast-jev-compaction](https://github.com/tamaratran/fast-jev-compaction) - Selects tool-call/result pairs to keep, truncate, or remove while preserving retained conversation content verbatim. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-jev-compaction.md).
- [fast-jev-opencode](https://github.com/nrdz-labs/fast-jev-opencode) - OpenCode V2 plugin: TypeSafe Jev prunes stale tool calls/results on the outgoing request (fail-open; leaves history/`/compact` alone). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-jev-opencode.md).
- [fast-jev-compaction-opencode](https://github.com/dex-community/fast-jev-compaction-opencode) - opencode verbatim session compaction via probabilistic keep/drop judgments (local LM Studio judge by default; based on fast-jev-compaction). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-jev-compaction-opencode.md).
- [fast-jev-opencode (roshan-shaik-ml)](https://github.com/roshan-shaik-ml/fast-jev-opencode) - OpenCode v1/v2 plugin: TypeSafe Jev prunes stale/bulky tool calls on the outgoing request (verbatim; distinct from nrdz-labs/fast-jev-opencode). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/roshan-shaik-ml-fast-jev-opencode.md).
- [FastGate](https://github.com/rajantripathi/fastgate-jev) - Multilingual EN/UZ/RU RAG helpdesk where TypeSafe Jev is the System One decision layer before the LLM writes, with an independent benchmark. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fastgate-jev.md).
- [Fastpunkt](https://github.com/Ritesist/fastpunkt) - Evidence-first research agent for Norwegian companies (built for the Builderr Signalpost challenge): given organisation numbers it returns one profile per company where every published fact links to its source with retrieval time; official registers anchor identity, and Jev (`jev-1.13.0`) is used only to select among candidates the code found (links, sentences, job titles) or to give a calibrated same-entity probability — no model-written facts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fastpunkt.md).
- [FAVA Trails](https://github.com/MachineWisdomAI/fava-trails) - Git-native, versioned memory for AI agents via MCP (Machine Wisdom AI): drafts are isolated, and since 0.8 the default Trust Gate asks TypeSafe Jev through OpenRouter one typed Noul question with an explicit threshold before a memory is promoted to shared truth. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fava-trails.md).
- [feelings](https://github.com/BoundaryML/feelings) - BAML `.feels()` / `.how()` / `.matches<T>()` typed AI-if methods powered by TypeSafe Jev; upstream licensing unspecified. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/feelings.md).
- [firefox-jev-mcp](https://github.com/kitoutou999/firefox-jev-mcp) - Claude plans; TypeSafe Jev picks Firefox clicks via MCP + WebExtension bridge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/firefox-jev-mcp.md).
- [Flick (flick-computer-use)](https://github.com/bgivenb/flick-computer-use) - Local MCP computer-use: agents hand Flick goals; TypeSafe Jev runs browser/macOS observe→decide→act. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/flick-computer-use.md).
- [fndds-matcher-jev](https://github.com/braydenabo/fndds-matcher-jev) - Match food descriptions to USDA FNDDS codes via hybrid retrieval plus TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fndds-matcher-jev.md).
- [Footwork](https://github.com/Tom-R-Main/Footwork) - Dual-process browser agent: TypeSafe Jev as System 1 in front of browser-use System 2, with a code-owned arbiter and evidence verification (`jevdual`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/footwork.md).
- [Foreman](https://github.com/thruwire/foreman) - Supervises Codex workers with Jev assessments and deterministic steering, retry, and verification policy, with a synthetic demo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/foreman.md).
- [forge (continuous engineering loop)](https://github.com/sudikama/forge) - Claude Code plugin that applies the autoresearch ratchet (one change, one commit, one measurement, keep or reset) inside a team-style process — spec, mapped scope, clarifying questions, tests beyond the happy path and human sign-off — with small tasks run through a test-driven harness and large ones driven to a measurable goal by an orchestrator, parallel subagents and a deterministic judge; Claude does the work while Jev makes cheap judgments such as triage, model routing and failure labelling. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/forge-claude-loop.md).
- [Formanator](https://github.com/timrogers/formanator) - CLI/MCP for Forma benefit claims; optional TypeSafe Jev chooses benefit/category when you supply claim fields (receipt inference stays on LLM). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/formanator.md).
- [fr (Arindam200)](https://github.com/Arindam200/fr) - Ask a codebase question and get relevant files via live parallel TypeSafe Jev relevance. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arindam200-fr.md).
- [fusion-jev](https://github.com/Tlkh201313/fusion-jev) - Local coding evidence plus optional guarded TypeSafe Jev choices for MCP hosts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fusion-jev.md).
- [Gatekeeper](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/gatekeeper) - YAML rules plus TypeSafe Jev Choice route Claude Code prompts to the right skill/agent (allow/route/block bands). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gatekeeper.md).
- [Gavel](https://github.com/shivanathd/gavel) - Salesforce packages for governed Choice/Score/Noul judgments with TypeSafe Jev, org policy, and an immutable ledger (v1 pre-release). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gavel.md).
- [gbrain-evals](https://github.com/garrytan/gbrain-evals) - Benchmark suite for the gbrain agent-memory system, including a matched-pair System One report on where TypeSafe Jev (`jev-1.13.0`) helps or hurts nine memory decision slots. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gbrain-evals.md).
- [genai (maruel)](https://github.com/maruel/genai) - High-performance multi-provider Go AI package whose `Provider.SystemOne()` asks typed decision questions across TypeSafe Jev, Cloudflare CLEF/CLEF-Flash, Ollama and local llama.cpp decision models with shared `genai.Questions` / `genai.Answers` maps. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/maruel-genai.md).
- [genigrep](https://github.com/Cloudgeni-ai/genigrep) - Ask a codebase question and get only answering source; TypeSafe Jev ranks candidates for coding agents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/genigrep.md).
- [ghtriage](https://github.com/KalyanM45/GitHub-Issue-Classification-Using-Jev) - Classifies GitHub issues with TypeSafe Jev typed labels and calibrated confidence; writes are opt-in (`ghtriage`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ghtriage.md).
- [git-jev-stage](https://github.com/ibrahemid/git-jev-stage) - Classifies Git hunks against a one-sentence staging intent with TypeSafe Jev, then stages confirmed blocks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/git-jev-stage.md).
- [gmail-auto-cleanup](https://github.com/nani-stack/gmail-auto-cleanup) - Python tool that sorts a Gmail inbox with a System One model — TypeSafe Jev (tested on ~27,000 emails) or experimental local Laya — answering typed yes/no questions per email while plain Python rules act; actions are archive + label with a 7-day hold before Trash, and the OAuth scope cannot permanently delete mail. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gmail-auto-cleanup.md).
- [go-decide](https://github.com/rshade/go-decide) - Go CLI that puts a choice or ordered rubric to a System One model — TypeSafe Jev by default, Cloudflare Clef opt-in — and prints a JSON outcome whose exit code tells your script to act, ask a person or escalate; it gates and labels decisions but never approves them. Early development, no tagged release yet. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/go-decide.md).
- [go-jev](https://github.com/mattn/go-jev) - Go SDK and jev-cli for TypeSafe Jev typed Noul/Choice/Score (explicit key; distinct from jevgo and TypeSafe Go). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/go-jev.md).
- [GoEventBus](https://github.com/Protocol-Lattice/GoEventBus) - High-performance Go event bus with deterministic rules, a decision cache, and an optional `JevSelector` that asks Jev (via OpenRouter's Decisions API) to choose an event type only on a cache miss. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/goeventbus.md).
- [goinfer](https://github.com/townsendmerino/goinfer) - Pure-Go, no-cgo local LLM inference (library and server) whose `POST /v1/systemone` answers TypeSafe's decisions wire shape so clients such as jevx work against it; it scores option labels with the served model or loads a trained decision head — TypeSafe's hosted model is not included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/goinfer.md).
- [gojev](https://github.com/taigrr/gojev) - Go harness for System One decision models — TypeSafe's hosted Jev, Jev via Vercel AI Gateway, Cloudflare Clef and in-process open-weight Kev — with typed Choice/Noul/Score question builders, calibrated answers, gating helpers and a CLI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gojev.md).
- [gpui-agent](https://github.com/stolinski/gpui-agent) - Jev-driven native GPUI testing over accessibility trees (experimental, macOS-first, instrumented apps). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gpui-agent.md).
- [graphify-jev](https://github.com/54Lynnn/graphify-jev) - Codebase knowledge graph with Tree-sitter AST topology plus TypeSafe Jev semantic navigation for coding agents (no vector DB). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/54lynnn-graphify-jev.md).
- [Graphlin](https://github.com/royosherove/graphlin) - Live architecture and activity diagrams for Claude Code or Codex while they explore code, with optional TypeSafe Jev classification. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/graphlin.md).
- [grev](https://github.com/aurorainfra/grev) - "Thinking coreutils": Unix filters (`grev`, `isv`, `tagv`, `rank`, `sortv`, `pickv`) that ask TypeSafe Jev typed questions, so output is always your input with labels or scores only as explicit columns. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grev.md).
- [Grok Bot Jev](https://github.com/Bodila51/grok-bot-jev) - Reference router and skill that use TypeSafe Jev to choose reuse/stop/cap/ask-human actions before expensive Grok Bot work. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grok-bot-jev.md).
- [grok-jev-guard](https://github.com/0xwhrari/grok-jev-guard) - Typed preflight/approval layer for Grok Bot: local policy plus TypeSafe Jev for ambiguity (shadow-first; distinct from Grok Bot Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grok-jev-guard.md).
- [grokbot-jev-skills](https://github.com/fal3/grokbot-jev-skills) - Ten Grok Bot agent skills, adapted from hermes-jev-skills, that hand small decisions — which search results to read, passage filtering and injection screening, mailbox sort, support triage, routine wake-ups, transcript cutting — to TypeSafe Jev through a `jev` CLI; fail-open, with documented redaction. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grokbot-jev-skills.md).
- [guardrails-md](https://github.com/berget-ai/guardrails-md) - Berget AI plugin for opencode, pi and Claude Code (plus a git pre-commit hook) that scores every bash command with a System One decision model against your repo's `guardrails.md` before it runs; defaults to Berget's `berget/bev` model, and any `/v1/systemone`-compatible endpoint (TypeSafe settings as fallback) can be used. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/guardrails-md.md).
- [guesswork](https://github.com/FindMalek/guesswork) - zsh history autosuggestions ranked by TypeSafe Jev instead of prefix match. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/guesswork.md).
- [Gut](https://github.com/vinibrsl/gut) - Elixir DSL for typed decisions in ordinary control flow (`Gut.feel/3` returns an Elixir value) with a `Gut.ReqLLM.Jev` adapter for TypeSafe Jev, plus other LLM, Ollama, and custom adapters. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gut.md).
- [gutfeel](https://github.com/huayaney-exe/gutfeel) - Usability testing for MCP servers: a participant that doesn't deliberate — TypeSafe Jev by default — walks your tools on realistic, hint-free missions, and gutfeel draws the tree of every path (right tool, wrong turn, dead end, error, loop) with the description sentence behind each failure; pre-alpha, with `gutfeel live` runnable today. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gutfeel.md).
- [gutsy](https://github.com/kouhxp/gutsy) - Local calibrated gutsy-0.8b decision model with Jev-compatible /v1/systemone and Decisions APIs on CPU (independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gutsy.md).
- [HA Jev Autopilot](https://github.com/davidzha712/ha-jev-autopilot) - Home Assistant: TypeSafe Jev decides per-room actions; code acts with ask-first for risky devices (distinct from HA-Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ha-jev-autopilot.md).
- [hak-jev-plugin](https://github.com/jmgomezl/hak-jev-plugin) - Hedera Agent Kit gate: TypeSafe Jev judges sensitive tool calls (v0 SaucerSwap) before sign; HCS receipts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hak-jev-plugin.md).
- [harness-router](https://github.com/Protocol-Lattice/harness-router) - Fast decision routing for agent harnesses — native MCP with Jev for tool selection and MCTS for multi-step decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/protocol-lattice-harness-router.md).
- [HearMemory](https://github.com/ssd1051/hearmemory) - Shared multi-agent coding memory: TypeSafe Jev checks claims against recorded evidence before the next agent trusts them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hearmemory.md).
- [HekaJev](https://github.com/zurk/hekajev) - Reproducible Git-history analytics powered by TypeSafe Jev classifications. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hekajev.md).
- [Herdr-Jev](https://github.com/flaviomartil/herdr-jev) - Plugin for the Herdr terminal workspace and AI-Harness that uses TypeSafe Jev System One (~260–340 ms) for multi-model triage — architectural complexity, research need and reasoning effort — then routes turns to Codex, Claude, Kiro and other peers with calibrated latency deadlines, Triad plan/implement/review orchestration, quota awareness and resumable runs. Separate from the listed alexzfe jev-herdr. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/herdr-jev.md).
- [here-we-go-jev](https://github.com/creativoma/here-we-go-jev) - Local playground/test bench for TypeSafe Jev: typed questions, calibrated probabilities, side-by-side LLM baseline, and mock backend (Bun). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/creativoma-here-we-go-jev.md).
- [Hermes Jev Skills](https://github.com/kerpopule/hermes-jev-skills) - Agent skills and `jev` CLI using TypeSafe Jev for model routing, memory filtering, compaction, skill selection, triage, and computer/browser action choice on Hermes, Claude Code, and Codex. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-jev-skills.md).
- [hermes-adaptive-model-router](https://github.com/INEEDBUG/hermes-adaptive-model-router) - Shadow-first adaptive model router plugin for Hermes Agent: a redacted, current-turn routing dossier goes to TypeSafe Jev, which chooses between a fast model and a high-capability model; decisions are logged content-free while the gateway's configured model still runs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-adaptive-model-router.md).
- [hermes-jev (ourines)](https://github.com/ourines/hermes-jev) - Hermes Agent decision sidekick: explicit Jev tools and a bundled skill via TypeSafe, Cloudflare, or OpenRouter backends. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ourines-hermes-jev.md).
- [hermes-jev-curator](https://github.com/anpicasso/hermes-jev-curator) - Hermes plugin: TypeSafe Jev typed skill-relationship judgments and safe curator archive/guard plans (experimental 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-jev-curator.md).
- [hermes-jev-helper](https://github.com/maurorosero/hermes-jev-helper) - Hermes Agent plugin: TypeSafe Jev intent classifier picks a source-first route (`skill`/`research`/…) before the LLM turn. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-jev-helper.md).
- [hermes-jev-lesson-gate](https://github.com/VBS2004/hermes-jev-lesson-gate) - Hermes plugin: TypeSafe Jev judges when memory/skill background review should run instead of a turn counter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-jev-lesson-gate.md).
- [himalaya-jev-mail-classify](https://github.com/initrd/himalaya-jev-mail-classify) - CLI that classifies Gmail threads via himalaya + TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter) and applies labels (dry-run default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/himalaya-jev-mail-classify.md).
- [HiRoute](https://github.com/higress-group/HiRoute) - Local-first agent routing engine with TypeSafe Jev decision extensions for branch selection. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hiroute.md).
- [Honeytongue](https://github.com/tbrought/honeytongue) - NPC persuasion engine: TypeSafe Jev judges whether player dialogue convinced a character. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/honeytongue.md).
- [hono-jev-router](https://github.com/yusukebe/hono-jev-router) - Experimental Hono router that matches HTTP requests to plain-English descriptions with TypeSafe Jev Noul judgments. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hono-jev-router.md).
- [hookgate](https://github.com/Allan-Nava/hookgate) - Claude Code/Codex hooks: TypeSafe Jev gates risky Bash and unverified completions (audit/fail-open). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hookgate.md).
- [hunch](https://github.com/steven-shoemaker/hunch) - Python verbs (`classify`/`score`/`check`/`pick`/`rank`/`where`) over scalars, lists, and pandas columns backed by TypeSafe Jev (`hunch-jev`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hunch.md).
- [hyalo](https://github.com/ractive/hyalo) - Rust CLI for Markdown knowledgebases (LLM Wiki pattern, frontmatter queries, links) whose `hyalo-tidy` agent skill can, when you explicitly ask, have TypeSafe Jev suggest missing types and existing folders for selected documents — audit-only suggestions the agent checks against local schemas. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hyalo.md).
- [imajev](https://github.com/mohit67890/imajev) - Open Jev-style multimodal typed-decision models (photos+records+text → options with can't-tell). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/imajev.md).
- [InspireJev](https://github.com/q1325833367/inspire-jev) - Browser-automation execution toolkit (Chinese and English docs) where the main agent plans and verifies while TypeSafe Jev picks Playwright actions from candidates actually observed on the page; GPT, Pi and MCP entries, with optional local `/v1/systemone` deciders in the 1.1 RC. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/inspire-jev.md).
- [Intent-Router](https://github.com/angel291592/Intent-Router) - Agent skill that compiles vague requests into typed IntentSpec contracts (probe/ask/halt) as input for Jev/Laya routers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/intent-router.md).
- [invalidate](https://github.com/chopratejas/invalidate) - Semantic TTL for agent memory: TypeSafe Jev checks every stored fact against new evidence; code marks superseded memories without rewriting text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/invalidate.md).
- [IronBee Express](https://github.com/ironbee-ai/ironbee-express) - Goal-driven browser agent: each step is one TypeSafe Jev decision over the page's offered controls plus one IronBee DevTools call that acts and returns the next snapshot, so model output never becomes a selector, coordinate or script; after the run it reviews the final page and API responses (and backend traces with IronBee), and runs can be recorded and replayed without the engine. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ironbee-express.md).
- [IronBee Gamer](https://github.com/ironbee-ai/ironbee-gamer) - Plays browser games live (2D canvas, Phaser, PixiJS, Cocos, HTML): a perception adapter turns what the page draws into a small JSON state and a decision engine picks every input — hosted TypeSafe Jev with your key, or Laya fine-tuned per game and run locally — with a web UI showing each state, action and probability, a coding-agent trainer for new games, and a shared Hugging Face library. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ironbee-gamer.md).
- [is-malicious](https://github.com/luantak/is-malicious) - Scans source/config/CI files with TypeSafe Jev for deceptive or data-stealing behavior and points to suspicious lines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/is-malicious.md).
- [J++](https://github.com/Towow-ai/jpp) - Experimental language and Rust runtime for composing semantic Jev questions with exact methods as values; offline fixtures included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jpp.md).
- [J3v](https://github.com/JGalego/J3v) - Edge-compiled System One decision runtime ('J3v is to Jev as k3s is to k8s') with Rust compiler/firmware demos. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/j3v.md).
- [japanese-jev-lint](https://github.com/pankona/japanese-jev-lint) - Go CLI (`jjl`) that flags Japanese sentences with TypeSafe Jev Noul probabilities (plus local です/ます regex); no rewrite generation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/japanese-jev-lint.md).
- [JarvisCore](https://github.com/Prescott-Data/jarviscore-framework) - Python multi-agent runtime (peer-to-peer mesh, durable state, integration atoms) with a `typesafe` extra that injects a shared Jev client as `self.decisions` and can route subagents, task complexity and RAG passages with Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jarviscore.md).
- [JCR](https://github.com/NiazMorshed2007/jcr) - Jev Capability Resolver: TypeSafe Jev searches a nested capability tree and returns deterministic command documentation for agents (MCP + harnesses). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jcr.md).
- [jear](https://github.com/iJ03l/jear) - Rust Jev-routed client for NEAR AI Cloud inference and IronClaw agents using budget/quality/sensitivity decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jear.md).
- [Jeeves (PostHog)](https://github.com/PostHog/jeeves) - Open 9B Jev-like reasoning classifier (noul/choice/score) with a Jev-compatible API and training code; independent of TypeSafe-hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/posthog-jeeves.md).
- [Jeff (firelex)](https://github.com/firelex/jeff) - Jeff: an open 0.8B "System 1" decision model (MIT code, Apache-2.0 weights on Hugging Face) that uses the same request format as Jev, with swappable LoRA adapters (guard, triage, tools, grounding, spam…), GGUF files for llama.cpp, and Jeff-Code; independent and not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/firelex-jeff.md).
- [jeff (Viperwow)](https://github.com/Viperwow/jeff) - Single-binary Rust classifier server with an admin page: one `/v1/systemone`-shaped API in front of Jev-compatible models — TypeSafe Jev in the cloud, a local Contrastive-LM (CLM) it installs, or Laya/PipeLLM/any compatible server — with access keys and a playground. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/viperwow-jeff.md).
- [Jeffort](https://github.com/andrewstephenson-v1/Jeffort) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev scores each prompt and sets Claude effort without touching the model (MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeffort.md).
- [jegrep](https://github.com/can1357/jegrep) - Semantic grep CLI: describe code intent and get Jev-ranked matches via TypeSafe or OpenRouter, without an embedding index. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jegrep.md).
- [Jev + Claude Code case study](https://github.com/gsaini/jev-claude-code-case-study) - Independent learning project testing what "Jev + Claude Code" means with two working programs: Triage Desk, a support-ticket app asking Jev six typed questions per ticket in one request with low-confidence cases going to human review, and Jev Guard, a Claude Code PreToolUse hook asking five redacted `noul` questions about ambiguous shell commands that never answers `allow`; both have offline evals and keyword baselines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-claude-code-case-study.md).
- [JEV Book Tags](https://github.com/iamjonatha/jev-book-tags) - Calibre plugin for AI-assisted book tagging with TypeSafe Jev (single-book and batch; GPL-3.0-or-later). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-book-tags.md).
- [jev Claude Code plugin (yinjs)](https://github.com/yinjs/claude-jev) - Claude Code plugin that hands small frequent decisions to Jev at six points — permission requests, prompt-injection warnings on fetched content, a 'done' re-check, prompt nudges, and jev-route effort/model routing — each one ~1 s call that changes nothing when Jev is unsure; Jev via OpenRouter (default), TypeSafe or Vercel AI Gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/yinjs-claude-jev.md).
- [Jev Engineering Cookbook](https://github.com/Jev-Engineering/cookbook) - Curriculum of Jupyter notebook recipes for building with Jev one bounded decision at a time — state in, typed `choice` / `noul` / `score` questions out — with offline fixture runs that need no API key, a live mode, and explicit provenance rules separating synthetic pipeline checks from measured Jev results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-engineering-cookbook.md).
- [Jev Filter (Apixly)](https://github.com/apixly-ai/jev-filter) - Semantic filtering for AI agent tools: npm package `@apixly/jev-filter` (CLI, Python library, MCP server) turns large tool outputs, retrieved records, code and logs into compact typed TypeSafe Jev decisions with source IDs, local originals and receipts before they enter an agent's context; batched requests and maintainer-run A/B benchmarks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apixly-jev-filter.md).
- [Jev for Claude](https://github.com/VoidLight00/jev-for-claude) - Dependency-free Claude Code plugin with nine user-invoked engineering skills (safety preflight, rule rubric, browser QA, evidence completion and more), conservative local hooks, and an explicit opt-in typed TypeSafe Jev judge adapter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/voidlight-jev-for-claude.md).
- [Jev for Excel](https://github.com/paulobrien/jev-excel) - Unofficial Excel add-in that calls TypeSafe Jev from worksheet formulas — `=JEV.ASK("choice"|"score"|"noul", A2, …)`, shortcut functions and `JEV.STATE` for multi-field rows — as an async Office add-in for Microsoft 365 or a Windows VBA edition. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-excel.md).
- [Jev Foundry Judge](https://github.com/nguyennhianhtri/jev-foundry-judge) - Drop-in Azure AI Foundry agent evaluators powered by TypeSafe Jev, plus a BYOK demo app and model-router bench. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-foundry-judge.md).
- [Jev Game Engine](https://github.com/King4s/jev-game-engine) - Early Rust desktop prototype for controlling games through bounded goals: TypeSafe Jev selects a goal, a local executor handles movement, validation and stopping, and every decision is traced, timed and replayable; Minecraft Java 26.2 is the first integration, with an offline fixture preview. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-game-engine.md).
- [Jev Graph Search](https://github.com/Emlembow/jev-graph-search) - CLI and agent skill that retrieves evidence from local Obsidian vaults, Logseq folders, or JSON memory snapshots: a local shortlist is reranked by Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-graph-search.md).
- [Jev Highlight Cutter](https://github.com/gnipbao/jev-highlight-cutter) - Codex agent skill and stdlib Python CLI (Chinese docs) that cuts long interviews, podcasts and streams into an evidence-backed highlight reel of a target length: transcript windows are scored by Jev against your preferences, selections are traceable, and FFmpeg makes a local rough cut. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-highlight-cutter.md).
- [jev-inbox-triage](https://github.com/pentasir/jev-inbox-triage) - Store-inbox triage where TypeSafe Jev answers eight parallel questions per email (topic, urgency, sentiment and more) and rules route it to one of four lanes with every decision logged; the author reports 19/20 test emails in the right lane and no risky email automated. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-inbox-triage.md).
- [Jev-Mod](https://github.com/i098/jev-mod) - Open-source Discord moderation bot where TypeSafe Jev rules detect spam, phishing, harassment, hate, threats and explicit text, with log/delete/timeout actions, local phrase and mention filters, exceptions and a case dashboard; use the hosted service with your own TypeSafe key or self-host. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mod.md).
- [Jev Model Router (Codex skill)](https://github.com/mrhsiung2015/jev-model-router) - Codex skill (Chinese and English docs) where the parent session condenses a non-trivial task and asks TypeSafe Jev to recommend the model tier and reasoning effort for an execution subagent; a standard-library Python script validates the answer against local policy and outputs one JSON decision, keeping the current model on timeout or invalid responses. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mrhsiung-jev-model-router.md).
- [Jev Moderation](https://github.com/abishakkodi/jev-moderation) - TypeScript and Python SDKs for text moderation with TypeSafe Jev: a bundled, versioned community policy or your own filters, each rule compiled to a Noul question, with review/block thresholds and a policy test CLI (validate, eval, compare with quality gates). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-moderation.md).
- [Jev Orchestrator](https://github.com/petercr/jev-orchestrator) - Small Node/TypeScript experiment testing whether Jev can make useful next-step decisions around a coding agent: decision-only by default (inspect the repo, send compact state to Jev via TypeSafe, Vercel or OpenRouter, apply deterministic thresholds, record a JSONL trace), with an explicit `--orchestrate` mode for a bounded, manually approved loop of safe searches, reads, read-only git, validation scripts and delegation to Codex CLI or Claude Code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-orchestrator.md).
- [jev plays snake](https://github.com/hoanganh25991/typesafe-jev) - Browser Snake where code computes legal moves and exact facts (food distance, flood-fill reachable cells, dead ends) and TypeSafe Jev picks one move per tick with a single `choice` question through a small Hono proxy; misses the deadline → the snake goes straight. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-plays-snake.md).
- [JEV Research MCP](https://github.com/Merth1470/jev-research-mcp) - Local-first MCP research tool: TypeSafe Jev filters search hits and page blocks before dumping them into an agent context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-research-mcp.md).
- [jevry](https://github.com/jeffrydegrande/jevry) - Small Go command-line tool for TypeSafe Jev: pipe text with `--question` to print the probability of yes, or send a full request JSON to print the raw response (defaults to `jev-latest`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevry.md).
- [Jev Skills (n23eos)](https://github.com/n23eos/jev-skills) - Fourteen installable Claude Code and Codex skills plus a CLI where TypeSafe Jev makes one bounded choice (skill, model, context file, first test, bug component or plan) and the coding agent does the work; distinct from the listed jev-skills plugin. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n23eos-jev-skills.md).
- [jev-a2a (Jev router for Paseo agents)](https://github.com/skhlo/jev-a2a) - Personal-prototype router that sends work between coding agents running in Paseo sessions on several machines and keeps one record of what was asked, who took it and what came back; when a request names no recipient, TypeSafe Jev chooses the participant. Includes a web board and design docs; published as a worked example, not a product. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-a2a.md).
- [jev-autopilot](https://github.com/hmcdaniel03/jev-autopilot) - Claude Code plugin that keeps unattended sessions moving: TypeSafe Jev answers Claude's routine questions, screens every non-read-only tool call, and holds risky ones until you tap Proceed or Block on your phone via Telegram; falls back to the terminal when Jev is unreachable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-autopilot.md).
- [jev-chess (byrencheema)](https://github.com/byrencheema/jev-chess) - Experiment where Jev plays chess by choosing one move from all legal moves as a single Choice per turn, with no search or filtering: 350 games vs Stockfish and Maia, 50 vs random, 1,000 Lichess puzzles, Stockfish analysis and all results committed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/byrencheema-jev-chess.md).
- [jev-claude-router (sergeiboikov)](https://github.com/sergeiboikov/jev-claude-router) - MCP server for Claude Code and Claude Desktop where TypeSafe Jev recommends which Claude model and effort level suit a task, comparing candidates by live OpenRouter prices, context sizes and output limits; it recommends only and never switches the model itself. Distinct from the separately listed jev-claude-router. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sergeiboikov-jev-claude-router.md).
- [jev-dev-demo (LIDR)](https://github.com/LIDR-academy/jev-dev-demo) - Spanish-language teaching repo with three runnable demos of 'Jev decides, the LLM reasons': Jira triage that classifies, prioritises and assigns each ticket with a confidence and escalates ambiguous ones, a pre-flight scan that flags existing bugs in the functions a ticket touches, and an LLM router that sends each PR to a large, small or no model (upstream reports 50–75% lower cost); every demo has a `--mock` mode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dev-demo.md).
- [jev-guardrail-benchmark](https://github.com/randilt/jev-guardrail-benchmark) - Benchmark of TypeSafe Jev content-safety guardrails in WSO2 AI Gateway vs Azure Content Safety and an LLM judge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-guardrail-benchmark.md).
- [jev-hook-guard](https://github.com/tsetse012/jev-hook-guard) - Standard-library Python module and example hook implementing the checks a Claude Code hook should run before sending session text to TypeSafe Jev or another HTTP judge: kill switch, exclusion words with session pins, refusal of secret dumps, masking of keys and personal data, length cut, leftover re-check, pending log line and an https-only, no-redirect POST. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-hook-guard.md).
- [jev-imdb-benchmark](https://github.com/rahulbansalc6414/jev-imdb-benchmark) - Benchmark TypeSafe Jev on IMDB reviews (sentiment/spoilers/quality) with published accuracy, latency, and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-imdb-benchmark.md).
- [jev-interceptor](https://github.com/rayopavri/jev-setup) - Claude Code `UserPromptSubmit` hook (repo `jev-setup`) that asks TypeSafe Jev a few typed questions about every prompt and tells Claude where the work should run — on Claude or an open-model lane (ClinePass via `pi`, or Codex) — returning the answer as `additionalContext`; a one-command installer also sets up companion skills, a fast Jev compaction plugin and a spend log, with OpenRouter as a backup path. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-interceptor.md).
- [jev-judge](https://github.com/00200200/jev-judge) - Pytest/Vitest-style CI evaluations of LLM and RAG outputs: declarative YAML tests (faithfulness, relevance, hallucination, guardrails) answered by TypeSafe Jev noul/score questions instead of an LLM-as-a-judge, with JSON/Markdown reports and an MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-judge.md).
- [jev-judge-rank](https://github.com/tsetse012/jev-judge-rank) - Three standard-library Python scripts (and an agent skill) that use TypeSafe Jev to rank a list on several written criteria, merge near-duplicates under one representative, and recall the corpus entries that help with a one-sentence situation; every request and response is logged locally, with a recorded example run and its costs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-judge-rank.md).
- [Jev-Lite](https://github.com/Atulmishra22/jev-lite) - Open System One–style FastAPI decision engine (Qwen backbone) inspired by TypeSafe Jev; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lite.md).
- [jev-map](https://github.com/krishhgg/jev-map) - Maps each Python function to the tests that run it for coding agents; optional TypeSafe Jev scores candidate links that static analysis misses, checked against real test runs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-map.md).
- [jev-max](https://github.com/saloni-garg/jev-max) - Jev browser agent aimed at pages ordinary Jev agents miss: a piercing snapshot walks open shadow roots and same-origin iframes into one indexed element table, a vision fallback handles canvas, clicks are occlusion-checked, one Jev request per cycle picks the operation and target (TypeSafe or OpenRouter), a small LLM writes text only for fill/select, and an independent Jev check verifies DONE; ships as a Python library, CLI and MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-max.md).
- [jev-mcp (drmedia)](https://github.com/drmedia/jev-mcp) - General-purpose MCP server for System One decision models: MCP clients ask typed yes/no, choice and score questions about structured state and get calibrated probabilities from TypeSafe Jev, OpenRouter (Jev, Cloudflare Clef) or a local server, with batching, retries, image input notes, stdio/HTTP transports and Docker. Distinct from the separately listed jev-mcp. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/drmedia-jev-mcp.md).
- [jev-mcp-router (ini8labs)](https://github.com/ini8labs/jev-mcp-router) - MCP gateway where TypeSafe Jev answers yes/no per server and tool, so the agent LLM only sees the tools a request needs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ini8labs-jev-mcp-router.md).
- [jev-mod (VictorGambarini)](https://github.com/VictorGambarini/jev-mod) - Claude Code mod that hands small decisions to a decision model — Jev via TypeSafe, OpenRouter, Venice or OpenCode Zen (or your own server): per-turn routing lanes that set effort and model, the one installed skill a prompt needs, screening of AI-directed instructions after WebFetch / WebSearch / MCP / fetching Bash, and `/compact-jev`, a summary-free compaction keeping only turns marked *keep*. Every decision fails open. Distinct from the listed Discord bot *Jev-Mod (undeemed)*. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/victorgambarini-jev-mod.md).
- [jev-navigator](https://github.com/ajbmachon/jev-navigator) - Python code-navigation library and `jvn` CLI for agents: a model-free index (definitions, callers, references, imports, git history) plus small typed Jev judgments — check, pick, score — composed into searches like “find the code this sentence describes”. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-navigator.md).
- [jev-netlify-mcp](https://github.com/NighCross/jev-netlify-mcp) - Two parts for using TypeSafe Jev from Claude Code, Codex CLI, Claude Desktop or any MCP client: a key-locked Netlify function that exposes `POST /v1/systemone` and `GET /v1/models` through Netlify AI Gateway (which injects the gateway credentials, so no TypeSafe key is needed), and an MCP server with `jev_evaluate` / `jev_models` tools plus question-writing instructions; it also works directly against TypeSafe with your own key. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-netlify-mcp.md).
- [jev-qa-demos](https://github.com/gauravkhuraana/jev-qa-demos) - TypeScript demos teaching QA/SDET engineers TypeSafe Jev via Vercel AI Gateway (triage, guardrail, local Ollama). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-qa-demos.md).
- [jev-query](https://github.com/jaketimothy/jev-query) - TypeScript natural-language-to-PostgreSQL composer where the model never writes SQL: code enumerates the legal moves of a typed QueryPlan, Jev (or a self-hosted logprob oracle) assigns probabilities, and code compiles parameterized SQL or asks a clarifying question. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-query.md).
- [jev-router (Covre81)](https://github.com/Covre81/JEV-APP-PC) - Local Anthropic-compatible gateway for Claude Code (or any Messages-API client): on each new human turn TypeSafe Jev answers one ordinal score, sending simple work to a cheap OpenAI-compatible provider (Ollama Cloud free plan by default, Groq or OpenRouter) and structural work byte-for-byte to Anthropic; tool-result turns reuse the route, Jev failures fall back to the primary, and an `x-jev-route` header plus statusline show each decision. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/covre81-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (Hoppin-Sorter)](https://github.com/Hoppin-Sorter/jev-router) - Before each prompt, asks TypeSafe Jev what the task is (difficulty, subject, output kind) and which of your skills fits; code then picks the Claude or OpenAI model and optional effort with a quality/cost focus slider. Ships as a Claude Code plugin, a TypeScript/Python router library, a Codex CLI launcher and an optional macOS menu-bar widget. Distinct from the separately listed jev-router. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hoppin-sorter-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (KarenCWLin1228)](https://github.com/KarenCWLin1228/jev-router) - Small Python CLI that asks TypeSafe Jev which model tier (fast / standard / deep) a coding task needs and returns a recommendation Claude Code, Codex or Antigravity can act on, plus a `jev-model-router` agent skill wiring it into those hosts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/karencwlin1228-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router-mcp](https://github.com/humayunkabir/jev-router-mcp) - MCP server that routes a question to the best tool via a Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` decision engine (works with Laya). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-router-mcp.md).
- [jev-search (larguesa)](https://github.com/larguesa/jev-search) - Complementary semantic search CLI and agent skill: Jev scores whether each candidate line expresses the intent you are looking for, so agents find by meaning what keyword search misses across repo docs, LLM wikis and Obsidian notes; in six simulated tasks lexical + Jev recovered 29 of 34 labeled passages vs 22 for lexical alone (author-reported). Not the listed jev-search app. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/larguesa-jev-search.md).
- [jev-secret-guard](https://github.com/BasmaAbouzied0/jev-secret-guard) - Claude Code hook: block known secret formats locally; send masked unknowns to TypeSafe Jev so checks never leak the secret. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-secret-guard.md).
- [jev-status](https://github.com/PeterFujiyu/jev-status-marketplace) - Claude Code plugin that asks TypeSafe Jev two independent questions after every turn — is the task done / needs action / failed, and is the work development- or production-ready — and shows both answers with probabilities above the prompt plus a toast. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-status.md).
- [jev-tokensaver](https://github.com/Dinesh-Sunny/jev-tokensaver) - Claude Code and Cowork pre-reader: a local `jev` CLI, MCP server and skill that let TypeSafe Jev read long test logs, big files and candidate-file lists (`prune`, `find`, `rank`, `map`) and pass Claude only the relevant lines — lossless, fail-open, secrets scrubbed locally. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-tokensaver.md).
- [JevTools](https://github.com/RileyCarney/JevTools) - Toolkit and reference implementation for Jev via OpenRouter decisions or the TypeSafe API: zero-dependency Python localhost server and web dashboard for review analysis, topic classification and custom decisions, local SQLite request history, and Markdown docs on control-inversion design. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtools.md).
- [jev-triage (OpenClaw)](https://github.com/CodeCave0000/jev-triage) - OpenClaw plugin that puts TypeSafe Jev in front of the agent's main model: each incoming message is classified thanks/spam/question/incident plus urgent yes/no, so thanks and spam end without waking the main model and other messages arrive with Jev's triage prepended; every decision is logged. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codecave-jev-triage.md).
- [jev-vs-clef](https://github.com/rmax-ai/jev-vs-clef) - Head-to-head stress comparison of TypeSafe Jev (via the Vercel AI Gateway) and Cloudflare Workers AI Clef (27B) / Clef-flash (9B) on synthetic, production-shaped decisions: an identical 159-call battery run in Clef's launch week and again on 2026-10-06, covering edge cases (64-question max, bad-image 422, vision, array state) and a wire gap around `noul` in the swap story, with raw calls and per-call cost published. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vs-clef.md).
- [jev-xray](https://github.com/viva-lee/jev-xray) - Sends every file in a repo to TypeSafe Jev with eight typed lenses in one request per file (role, blast radius, complexity, security-sensitive, tech debt, deserves tests, start here, legacy) and renders a live treemap; ask a new question to relight all files, drill down to the line Jev ranks highest, and read 'doubt' (1 − mean confidence) as a reading list. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-xray.md).
- [jev-zh-tw-eval](https://github.com/wuchengwei1996/jev-zh-tw-eval) - Traditional Chinese (zh-TW) evaluation of Jev-compatible System One models (Plumb-4B via Ollama), including adversarial probes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-zh-tw-eval.md).
- [Jev.DotNet (altinburak)](https://github.com/altinburak/Jev.DotNet) - Unofficial .NET 8+ client for TypeSafe Jev published on NuGet as `Jev.DotNet`: typed Choice/Score/Noul questions and answers, enum-backed choices, retries with backoff, timeouts, logging and `IHttpClientFactory` / ASP.NET Core dependency-injection support. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dotnet-altinburak.md).
- [jev4pg](https://github.com/Sheltercosmo/jev4pg) - Natural-language-to-SQL and semantic operators for PostgreSQL: TypeSafe Jev answers typed questions per record while PostgreSQL handles joins and aggregation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev4pg.md).
- [JEV_accel](https://github.com/poyen-tseng/jev-accel) - Traditional-Chinese Python package, CLI and MCP server that speeds up embedded bench work on a TI F280049C board with a RIGOL MSO5104 oscilloscope and Code Composer Studio: deterministic L0 rules answer first, TypeSafe Jev (`typesafe-sdk` Choice/Noul) is called only for ambiguous cases such as prioritising multiple violations, and the agent gets measurement state, a verdict and ready-to-send SCPI or next commands in one tool call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-accel.md).
- [jevai](https://github.com/tiendvlp/jevai) - Unofficial async Rust client for TypeSafe System One (noul/choice/score) against docs.typesafe.ai. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevai.md).
- [jev-cli (shetautnetjer)](https://github.com/shetautnetjer/jev-cli) - Clean-room CLI for TypeSafe System One Decision Contracts, search, and benchmarks (distinct from tumf/shaharia-lab jev-cli). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/shetautnetjer-jev-cli.md).
- [JEV Codex Pilot](https://github.com/Charlyhno-eng/jev-codex-pilot) - Local Codex Kanban workspace with Jev-model routing, contextual compaction, and ticket lifecycle control. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-codex-pilot.md).
- [jev-compaction-plus](https://github.com/cth9191/jev-compaction-plus) - Claude Code compaction via TypeSafe Jev keep/drop (~0.5s): retained text stays verbatim; dropped tool outputs move to a drawer file (fork of fast-jev-compaction). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-compaction-plus.md).
- [Jev Cookbook (Datawhale)](https://github.com/datawhalechina/jev-cookbook) - Chinese System One/Jev cookbook: notebooks, 18 recipes, docs translation (CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 — free educational / non-commercial; not OSI open source). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cookbook.md).
- [jev-decision-kit (kdcadmin)](https://github.com/kdcadmin/jev-decision-kit) - Local skill/plugin cabinet: an on-device Jev head selects which skills to preface before the chat model speaks (distinct from DecisionKit .NET). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kdcadmin-jev-decision-kit.md).
- [jev-doc-search](https://github.com/VectifyAI/jev-doc-search) - Find answering pages in long PDFs with TypeSafe Jev Choice over a PageIndex tree (no vector DB). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-doc-search.md).
- [jev-dotnet (CMaintz)](https://github.com/CMaintz/jev-dotnet) - Unofficial zero-dependency .NET client for TypeSafe Jev Choice/Score/Noul (distinct from ukashanoor/jev-dotnet). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cmaintz-jev-dotnet.md).
- [Jev for Claude Code (brookcs3)](https://github.com/brookcs3/jev--system-one-for-claude) - Unofficial Claude Code plugin exposing TypeSafe Jev typed judgments (noul/choice/score) plus corpus→eval loops. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/brookcs3-jev-system-one-for-claude.md).
- [jev-gateway (TexasOct)](https://github.com/TexasOct/jev-gateway) - OpenAI-compatible session-aware model-routing gateway with optional typed Choice decision providers (AGPL; distinct from vinilana/jev-gateway). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/texasoct-jev-gateway.md).
- [jevb](https://github.com/mitya777/jevb) - Browser and phone use for AI agents in plain English: the agent says what it wants (`jevb act mark "Buy oat milk" as done`, `jevb check …`) and TypeSafe Jev picks the element or judges the screen in about 150–300 ms, driving headless Chromium, your own Chrome, or real iOS and Android phones and returning short JSON instead of page snapshots. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevb.md).
- [JevBird](https://github.com/leftspace89/JevBird) - Pygame Flappy Bird where TypeSafe Jev picks one of several simulated flight paths through each pipe gap (roughly one API call per pipe) and the game draws the chosen and rejected paths. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevbird.md).
- [jevdedup](https://github.com/Ruivalim/jevdedup) - Bun CLI that finds duplicate files with size, quick-hash and SHA-256 checks, then asks Jev to confirm each group, flag mislabeled files, suggest which copy to keep and judge near-duplicate pairs; reports only, never deletes; self-described exercise project. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdedup.md).
- [JevGB](https://github.com/SuperfastSimon/jevgb) - Unofficial community extension of the listed grok-bot-jev: before Grok Bot does something expensive it calls `jevgb route` with a short JSON state and a router picks one action (e.g. hand off code work to Grok Build as a compact brief, or reuse a cached artifact), logging what each decision cost in a token ledger and dashboard; the default classifier is deterministic rules, with Jev used when `TYPESAFE_API_KEY` and `typesafe-sdk` are present. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevgb.md).
- [jevkit (bragamat)](https://github.com/bragamat/jevkit) - Unofficial `jev` CLI (Homebrew, Scoop, install script) that puts TypeSafe Jev in a coding agent's toolbox: `find`/`check` to read less, `decide`/`yesno`/`score` with ACT/CONFIRM/REPHRASE verdicts tuned to risk, and a local gateway that predicts the next tool for Claude Code or Codex; distinct from the other jevkit listing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bragamat-jevkit.md).
- [Jevlint (codegirl-007)](https://github.com/codegirl-007/jevlint) - Go linter that checks code against plain-language team rules: Tree-sitter extracts code units and TypeSafe Jev judges whether each passes (distinct from huntedman/JevLint and the jev-lint projects). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codegirl-007-jevlint.md).
- [jevmem (illescasDaniel)](https://github.com/illescasDaniel/jev-mem) - Agent long-term memory via TypeSafe Jev typed decisions (MCP, Claude Code hooks, CLI, SQLite)—no generative LLM in the memory loop (MIT; distinct from libingzheren/Jev-Mem). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/illescasdaniel-jev-mem.md).
- [Jev Mode](https://github.com/tiffygk/jev-mode) - Claude Code skills and study course for building with TypeSafe Jev (PolyForm Noncommercial; not all skills call Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tiffygk-jev-mode.md).
- [Jev Observer](https://github.com/LimePencil/jev-observer) - Local proxy and dashboard for TypeSafe Jev decisions (history, versions, latency/cost) with an embedded web UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-observer.md).
- [Jevometry](https://github.com/Kunyanli230/Jevometry) - Information-geometric toolkit for System One (Jev/Jevlike) decision probabilities—Fisher sensitivity over declared models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevometry.md).
- [jev-permission-gate (madisonrickert)](https://github.com/madisonrickert/jev-permission-gate) - Claude Code auto-mode tool-call gate using TypeSafe Jev to allow, deny, or defer to the built-in classifier. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/madisonrickert-jev-permission-gate.md).
- [jev-pr-quality](https://github.com/rawtreedb/jev-pr-quality) - GitHub Action for TypeSafe Jev-assisted pull request quality reviews with a live multi-repository RawTree dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-pr-quality.md).
- [jevroute](https://github.com/maxkulish/jevroute) - Claude Code skill-hint hook via TypeSafe Jev—STOPPED after baseline pilot (69/75 without hints); published negative findings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevroute.md).
- [Jev Router for Windows](https://github.com/pouramin/jev-router-windows) - One-click Windows GUI for TypeSafe Jev routing with Codex (jev-codex-bridge) and assisted Claude Code plugin setup. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-router-windows.md).
- [jev-router (hectorj2f)](https://github.com/hectorj2f/jev-router) - Route Claude Code subagents by Jev depth/breadth/noul scores; publishes where the idea fails (distinct from other jev-router*). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hectorj2f-jev-router.md).
- [jev-supervisor](https://github.com/ryyyzer/jev-supervisor) - DeepSeek Harness macOS plugin that supervises agent execution with TypeSafe Jev decisions and budgets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-supervisor.md).
- [jev-tools (Rcidshacker)](https://github.com/Rcidshacker/jev-tools) - Claude Code plugin: OpenJev/Codiv for cheap typed agent judgments (edit hook, skill picker, review pre-check); distinct from CMaintz/jev-tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rcidshacker-jev-tools.md).
- [JevAlt](https://github.com/mertkayacs/jevalt) - Open Jev-API–compatible Choice/Score/Noul models (EN/TR/DE) for CPU (~4 GB RAM); independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevalt.md).
- [jev-answers](https://github.com/AmooEbrahim/jev-answers) - MCP server + CLI: send files and typed questions to TypeSafe Jev and get the raw answer back (one session folder per request). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-answers.md).
- [jev-cc-codex-router](https://github.com/peterwanghot/jev-cc-codex-router) - Per-turn Codex model-routing proxy: TypeSafe Jev picks tier, rewrites the model, retries flaky upstream errors (distinct from 0xNatoshi/jev-codex-router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cc-codex-router.md).
- [jev-cops](https://github.com/FrancoisChastel/jev-cops) - Context-aware tool-call policing for coding agents with graduated Jev-backed verdicts (allow→kill). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cops.md).
- [jev (drpaneas)](https://github.com/drpaneas/jev) - Go package for calling TypeSafe Jev System One typed questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/drpaneas-jev.md).
- [jev-dotnet](https://github.com/ukashanoor/jev-dotnet) - Unofficial typed C# client for the Jev System One API (Choice/Score/Noul). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dotnet.md).
- [jev-gateway-bench](https://github.com/vinilana/jev-gateway-bench) - Benchmark suite for vinilana/jev-gateway: coding agents on chess-engine tasks with Jev routing on vs off. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gateway-bench.md).
- [jev-herdr](https://github.com/alexzfe/jev-herdr) - Spawn Claude Code agents in Herdr with TypeSafe Jev choosing model and effort per agent. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-herdr.md).
- [jev-llm-guard](https://github.com/gulbaki/jev-llm-guard) - Contextual OWASP LLM Top 10 guardrail powered by TypeSafe Jev, with a Turkish interactive demo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-llm-guard.md).
- [jev (okooo5km)](https://github.com/okooo5km/jev) - Stdlib Python CLI + Agent Skill for TypeSafe Jev `yes`/`pick`/`score` via TypeSafe API or OpenRouter (distinct from typesafe-cli / typesafeai-cli). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/okooo5km-jev.md).
- [jev-php (f-lombardo)](https://github.com/f-lombardo/jev-php) - PHP client library for TypeSafe Jev / System One APIs (LGPL-2.1). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/f-lombardo-jev-php.md).
- [jev (polidog)](https://github.com/polidog/jev) - Unofficial Rust CLI for TypeSafe Jev across TypeSafe, Cloudflare, and Vercel providers (CLI shape follows stefafafan/jev)—distinct from feder-cr/jev (jevos). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/polidog-jev.md).
- [jev (stefafafan)](https://github.com/stefafafan/jev) - Unofficial provider-neutral Go/Unix CLI for TypeSafe Jev (Choice/Noul/Score) via TypeSafe, Cloudflare, or Vercel. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stefafafan-jev.md).
- [jev (taifoon-io)](https://github.com/taifoon-io/jev) - Grade an AI agent job with TypeSafe Jev: fact checks in code, four closed questions, receipt with probabilities, optional on-chain records (`@taifoon/jev`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/taifoon-io-jev.md).
- [JEV ADK](https://github.com/abyakod/JEV_ADK) - Agent Development Kit patterns for TypeSafe Jev: guardrails, dual-brain routing, and PR triage blueprints (System One decisions in code). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-adk.md).
- [Jev Browser (openqa-cn)](https://github.com/openqa-cn/jev-browser) - Indexed Playwright automation: TypeSafe Jev chooses control/op; CodexQA skill with replay and HTML reports. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openqa-jev-browser.md).
- [Jev Browser (tontoko)](https://github.com/tontoko/jev-browser) - Integrates Jev field selection and source-backed extraction with a Playwright SDK, CLI, MCP server, and explicit assertions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-tontoko.md).
- [Jev Browser (Ying-Kai-Liao)](https://github.com/Ying-Kai-Liao/jev-browser) - Turns caller-defined browser goals into Jev-selected Playwright actions with a library, CLI, and MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-ying-kai-liao.md).
- [Jev Browser Skill](https://github.com/zurfyx/jev-browser-skill) - Claude Code/Codex skill where TypeSafe Jev chooses browser operations and targets from a code-built element table (reference implementation; explainer site included). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-skill.md).
- [Jev Bug Hunter](https://github.com/BillNDD/jev-bug-hunter) - Bounded first-pass bug hunting for source files powered by TypeSafe Jev — a quick sanity check, not a replacement for tests or review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-bug-hunter.md).
- [Jev by Example](https://github.com/ReallyArtificial/jev-by-example) - Ten runnable JavaScript lessons on agent decisions between steps; Jev supplies typed judgments and code owns policy (offline fixtures by default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-by-example.md).
- [Jev Classification for n8n](https://github.com/khmuhtadin/n8n-nodes-jev-classification) - Adds typed classification, scoring, yes/no checks, and batched questions to self-hosted n8n, with configurable review routing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-classification-n8n.md).
- [Jev Classifier for Obsidian](https://github.com/ItsBen321/obsidian-jev-classifier) - Obsidian plugin: classify note properties with TypeSafe Jev using an editable guide (one note or whole vault). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/obsidian-jev-classifier.md).
- [Jev Code Reviewer (egma-ai)](https://github.com/egma-ai/jev-code-reviewer) - Local PR triage: TypeSafe Jev prioritizes changes; OpenAI explains; Chrome overlay posts nothing to GitHub. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/egma-ai-jev-code-reviewer.md).
- [Jev Decision Gateway (kartikanand73)](https://github.com/kartikanand73/jev-decision-gateway) - Governed withdrawal PoC: TypeSafe Jev scores, deterministic policy signs, executor only accepts signed decisions; bench vs GPT-6 Sol and rules — distinct from kuldeepsinh19/jev-decision-gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kartikanand73-jev-decision-gateway.md).

- [JEV Flaky Detective](https://github.com/JevForge/jev-flaky-detective) - GitHub Action: TypeSafe Jev classifies failing tests as regression/flaky/environment/unknown (never auto-reruns). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-flaky-detective.md).
- [Jev Flow](https://github.com/daltonrpj/jev-flow) - Standalone Node.js studio for typed Jev workflows (Studio, Compendium, Battle Arena; distinct from jev-flow-harness). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-flow.md).

- [Jev Gatehouse (Kinde)](https://github.com/kinde-starter-kits/jev-agent-authorization) - Kinde identity + permissions with TypeSafe Jev typed gates before every MCP tool call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gatehouse.md).

- [Jev Lab](https://github.com/jammaru/jev-lab) - Local Hundred NPC-town and Jev Shogi labs where TypeSafe Jev chooses the next legal action (Rules mode without a key). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lab.md).

- [JEV Mail Filtering](https://github.com/albertcas/jev-mail-filtering) - Local read-only IMAP inbox triage with TypeSafe Jev (needs reply / worth reading / scam / unsure). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mail-filtering.md).
- [Jev MCP (Freepik)](https://github.com/freepik-company/jev-mcp) - Go MCP server for typed decide/classify/verify/rerank with Jev via OpenRouter or TypeSafe (binary/container releases). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/freepik-jev-mcp.md).
- [Jev MCP Server (keysersoft)](https://github.com/keysersoft/jev-mcp-server) - AnythingMCP connector exposing TypeSafe Jev yes/no, classification, and scoring tools to Claude, ChatGPT, and other MCP hosts (hosted or self-hosted). - [jev-compaction (Waxmell114514)](https://github.com/Waxmell114514/jev-compaction) - Context compactor that can only score, never write—so agent memory cannot invent transcript facts (offline demo; distinct from fast-jev-compaction). - [jev-computer-use-skill (Mrchen116)](https://github.com/Mrchen116/jev-computer-use-skill) - Codex/agent skill that delegates repetitive computer-use judgments to TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mrchen116-jev-computer-use-skill.md).
- [jev-controller](https://github.com/ac-kurniawan/jev-controller) - Fail-open advisory post-tool controller for OMP (oh-my-pi): asks TypeSafe Jev whether to inspect, edit, test, or ask the user—leaves the tool result unchanged if Jev is unavailable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ac-kurniawan-jev-controller.md).
- [jev-corrective-rag](https://github.com/sudeshkar/jev-corrective-rag) - Corrective RAG demo: TypeSafe Jev typed gates for triage/grading/verify; generative LLM only writes answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-corrective-rag.md).
- [Jev-cu](https://github.com/Sac-Y/Jev-cu) - Experimental Codex skill and JavaScript runtime for selecting macOS Accessibility targets with Jev, with action previews and optional result verification; execution-policy limitations require review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cu.md).
- [jev-debtgate](https://github.com/smlayero/jev-debtgate) - Technical-debt gate for coding agents and CI: local metrics plus TypeSafe Jev typed questions and confidence policy (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-debtgate.md).
- [jev-decide (abdullahaamuda)](https://github.com/abdullahaamuda-code/jev-decide) - Agent skill + stdlib Python CLI: offload page-state Choice/Noul/Score judgments to TypeSafe Jev for browser/desktop automation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/abdullahaamuda-jev-decide.md).
- [jev-decisions (wonghanz)](https://github.com/wonghanz/jev-decisions) - Typed decision layer for backend engineering (log triage, incident routing, PR triage, deploy risk) with a control-group eval harness via OpenRouter Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/wonghanz-jev-decisions.md).
- [jev-dom](https://github.com/eralabs-ai/jev-dom) - TypeSafe Jev operates any web page through its DOM action space (no WebMCP required); Playwright peer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dom.md).
- [jev-drone](https://github.com/RomanSlack/jev-drone) - Explores Jev tactical judgments in a MuJoCo quadrotor simulation, with local flight control and a separate experimental tunnel policy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-drone.md).
- [jev-effort](https://github.com/ifoster01/jev-effort) - Unofficial Claude Code helper: TypeSafe Jev picks per-step reasoning effort and lease (OpenRouter/TypeSafe/Vercel). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-effort.md).
- [jev-effort-router](https://github.com/AlphaPerseii3000/jev-effort-router) - Hermes plugin for Ollama:Cloud: TypeSafe Jev routes both model and reasoning effort per turn via `llm_request` rewrite. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-effort-router.md).
- [jev-enforce](https://github.com/erkamyaman/jev-enforce) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev checks every reply and file edit against CLAUDE.md / AGENTS.md rules and blocks broken rules in-turn. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-enforce.md).
- [jev-eyes](https://github.com/LeddoEngano/jev-eyes) - Local OCR/layout `see()` (and optional `ask()`) so TypeSafe Jev decides over inspectable image-derived text state. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-eyes.md).
- [jev-fanout-bench](https://github.com/blowxian/jev-fanout-bench) - Reproducible measurement that N TypeSafe Jev questions in one call bill shared state once (2,976 requests, raw JSONL). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-fanout-bench.md).
- [jev-feels](https://github.com/Qew7/jev-feels) - Ruby gem for TypeSafe Jev as `feels?` / `decide` / `score` and Rails validations (distinct from BAML feelings and ruby_decision_model). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-feels.md).
- [jev-filter (ByteBell)](https://github.com/ByteBell/jev-filter) - Claude Code plugin: after grep, TypeSafe Jev noul-filters which files matter (~1% of LLM file-triage cost per upstream bench). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bytebell-jev-filter.md).
- [jev-flight-agent](https://github.com/kallurayaankit/jev-flight-agent) - Browser-use flight search demo: TypeSafe Jev picks DOM actions; small LLM types text only (Playwright/CDP). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-flight-agent.md).
- [jev-for-all](https://github.com/emirbartu/jev-for-all) - Shared TypeSafe Jev decision contract for OpenCode, Claude Code, and Hermes (skill routing, tool subset, browser). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-for-all.md).

- [jev-fuse](https://github.com/0xshikhar/jev-fuse) - Governed execution/safety-gate/audit proxy for TypeSafe Jev and local Laya engines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-fuse.md).

- [jev-gate (Neoo-Blue)](https://github.com/Neoo-Blue/jev-gate) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev checks the plan vs your request before edits, then reviews the final summary until gaps are closed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/neoo-blue-jev-gate.md).
- [jev-gates](https://github.com/carlchou0dailyfresh/jev-gates) - Composable three-valued semantic logic circuits from TypeSafe Jev judgments and exact rules, with mock/offline demos (distinct from typesafe-agent-gates). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gates.md).
- [jev-gateway](https://github.com/vinilana/jev-gateway) - Local LLM gateway that asks TypeSafe Jev which tool to call for Codex, Claude Code, OpenCode, or Gemini while other traffic uses your usual LLM. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gateway.md).

- [jev-guard (klauswg)](https://github.com/klauswg/jev-guard) - Exchange deposit/withdrawal risk triage: TypeSafe Jev judges; Java code adjudicates (distinct from leepokai/CMaintz jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/klauswg-jev-guard.md).
- [jev-guard (muratcakmak)](https://github.com/muratcakmak/jev-guard) - Claude Code hooks: TypeSafe Jev probability-scored rules deny bad edits/deploys; fail-open if scorer unavailable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/muratcakmak-jev-guard.md).
- [jev-guard (rudra72r)](https://github.com/rudra72r/jev-guard) - Python LLM-app guardrails library: TypeSafe Jev (or local/Cloudflare backends) scores inputs/outputs with allow/review/block policies—distinct from leepokai/jev-guard agent hooks. - [jev-router (jjjjjjjjjjjjjjjjacob)](https://github.com/jjjjjjjjjjjjjjjjacob/jev-router) - Claude Code plugin + skills: Jev picks effort level for every prompt and the right model for every subagent; off until you type /jev. Distinct from other jev-router listings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jjjjjjjjjjjjjjjjacob-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (peptidehackers)](https://github.com/peptidehackers/jev-router) - Numpy-only System One decision heads with Wilson-certified act-or-escalate routing (SalesRLAgent prior art)—independent of hosted Jev; distinct from gargpratyush/jev-router. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/peptidehackers-jev-router.md).
- [jev-rules](https://github.com/EliaAlberti/jev-rules) - Selects Claude Code project rules and map documents with Jev judgments over prompts and file paths, with session caching and fallback context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-rules.md).
- [jev-sap-commerce](https://github.com/Emenowicz/jev-sap-commerce) - SAP Commerce extension: TypeSafe Jev review moderation + category suggestions with dry runs and audit records. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/emenowicz-jev-sap-commerce.md).
- [jev-sdk-java](https://github.com/luigivis/jev-sdk-java) - Unofficial Java 21 System One client with sealed Question/Answer types (TypeSafe-only; source-build until Maven Central lists 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sdk-java.md).
- [jev-search (AnthonyDavidAdams)](https://github.com/AnthonyDavidAdams/jev-search) - Agentic search library/CLI: deterministic fetch/parse; only link/candidate decisions go to Jev (or local Laya). Distinct from superagents-lab Jev Search app. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/anthonydavidadams-jev-search.md).
- [jev-seatbelts](https://github.com/LocoLoboZ/jev-seatbelts) - Seven Claude Code hooks that catch expensive agent mistakes; TypeSafe Jev on judgment tiers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-seatbelts.md).
- [jev-sec-audit](https://github.com/DhanushNehru/jev-sec-audit) - Lightning-fast AI supply-chain security auditor: TypeSafe Jev scores typosquatting and malicious package scripts in milliseconds (CLI + GitHub Action). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dhanushnehru-jev-sec-audit.md).
- [jev-sec-bench](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/jev-sec-bench) - Blind TypeSafe Jev security benchmarks (prompt injection + vulnerable code) on public corpora, with a results TUI (built on jev-go). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sec-bench.md).
- [jev-security-prioritization](https://github.com/san3ncrypt3d/jev-security-prioritization) - Experiment: TypeSafe Jev prioritizes SCA/SAST findings with context vs severity-only baselines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-security-prioritization.md).
- [jev-security-sentinel](https://github.com/JevForge/jev-security-sentinel) - GitHub Action that gates SAST/SCA/IaC/secrets/container findings with TypeSafe Jev (`PASS`/`WARN`/`BLOCK`/`REVIEW`) without hiding findings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-security-sentinel.md).
- [jev-seo](https://github.com/AkashPriyadarshii/jev-seo) - Agent-oriented SEO/GEO CLI and MCP with local DuckDuckGo helpers and optional TypeSafe Jev scoring. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-seo.md).
- [jev-seo (AgriciDaniel)](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/jev-seo) - Live website SEO audit from one URL with TypeSafe Jev judgments and PDF/XLSX/Markdown reports (distinct from AkashPriyadarshii/jev-seo). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agrici-jev-seo.md).
- [jev-seo-skills](https://github.com/jackson7705/jev-seo-skills) - Five tested SEO workflows powered by TypeSafe Jev: intent, internal links, cannibalization, brief QA, AI mention tracking. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jackson7705-jev-seo-skills.md).
- [jev-shadow-adapter](https://github.com/ianlintner/jev-shadow-adapter) - Shadow-mode observational adapter harness for TypeSafe Jev routing decisions, with an MkDocs GitHub Pages site. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ianlintner-jev-shadow-adapter.md).
- [jev-shell-history](https://github.com/mrnugget/jev-shell-history) - Ranks recent zsh commands with Jev for inline suggestions; sends selected history to TypeSafe and requires a user-supplied API key. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-shell-history.md).
- [jev-shield](https://github.com/caiovicentino/jev-shield) - Semantic MCP firewall and skill: TypeSafe Jev (Vercel AI Gateway) screens tool calls, results, and descriptions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-shield.md).
- [jev-sim-use](https://github.com/Ryu0118/jev-sim-use) - iOS Simulator/Android navigator: TypeSafe Jev picks the next on-screen action each step (sim-use). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sim-use.md).
- [jev-skill (marcodicesare-dev)](https://github.com/marcodicesare-dev/jev-skill) - Community Jev skill for Claude Code/Codex: guide, Python CLI, five tested recipes, and measured findings from ~19k API calls (not TypeSafe's official skill). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/marcodicesare-dev-jev-skill.md).
- [jev-skill-gate](https://github.com/ShivamPansuriya/jev-skill-gate) - Gates Claude Code skill manifests with TypeSafe Jev relevance scores and `skillOverrides` so irrelevant skills stay out of context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skill-gate.md).
- [jev-skill-router-bench](https://github.com/OrMizL/jev-skill-router-bench) - Independent measurement of a Jev skill router on a Hermes roster (published scorecard; not a vendor replica). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skill-router-bench.md).
- [jev-skill-scout](https://github.com/karanb192/jev-skill-scout) - Finds Claude Code turns where a skill should have loaded and did not (TypeSafe Jev audit CLI + live suggest mod). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skill-scout.md).
- [jev-skills](https://github.com/eran-broder/jev-skills) - Claude Code/Codex plugin: TypeSafe Jev selects which installed skills enter context each turn (zero always-on skill-list tokens). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skills.md).
- [jev-studio](https://github.com/utk2103/jev-studio) - Python CLI (`jev`) plus MCP server kit for TypeSafe Jev verify/screen/classify/extract flows and cookbook tools (Alpha 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-studio.md).
- [Jev-Style](https://github.com/lawrence3699/jev-style) - Local calibrated decision models with systemone-compatible server, agent skills, Claude Code guard, and MCP tools; HF weights—independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-style.md).
- [jev-subtitle-translator](https://github.com/GeekLinkDev/jev-subtitle-translator) - Translate SRT subtitles with structured LLM output and TypeSafe Jev QC checks (GPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-subtitle-translator.md).
- [jev-suite](https://github.com/klauswg/jev-suite) - Four Java decision-quality apps on one TypeSafe Jev kernel; deterministic code keeps thresholds and vetoes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-suite.md).
- [jev-support-agents](https://github.com/RavioliCodes/jev-support-agents) - FastAPI multi-agent support reference: LLMs draft replies; TypeSafe Jev routes, evaluates, and retries/escalates (60-ticket bench). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-support-agents.md).
- [jev-swap](https://github.com/0xjba/jev-swap) - Scan codebases for decision-shaped LLM calls, generate TypeSafe Jev modules, and shadow-test savings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-swap.md).
- [jev-switch](https://github.com/ARCJ137442/jev-switch) - Local-first Jev protocol gateway (Rust/axum + React/Tauri): aggregate upstreams, editable routing DAG, playground—Vercel/Laya adapters today; TypeSafe/OpenRouter on the roadmap. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arcj137442-jev-switch.md).
- [jev-switchboard](https://github.com/ZIJIAN004/jev-switchboard) - Semantic gate for parallel Claude Code/Codex agents: TypeSafe Jev chooses interrupt vs drop and which evidence to inject. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-switchboard.md).
- [jev-table](https://github.com/dtduc-git/jev-table) - Local-first CLI that adds TypeSafe Jev AI columns to CSV/JSONL with confidence, review queue, resume, and dry-run cost preview. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-table.md).
- [jev-test-filter](https://github.com/mizchi/jev-test-filter) - Scores each test against a git diff with TypeSafe Jev and emits filter args for vitest, Jest, node:test, Playwright, cargo, and go test. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-test-filter.md).
- [jev-test-impact](https://github.com/holasoymalva/jev-test-impact) - Test impact analysis CLI/Action: Git diff + static deps + TypeSafe Jev score candidate tests so CI runs only likely-affected Vitest/Jest files. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-test-impact.md).
