- [jev-test-triage](https://github.com/cdubiel08/jev-test-triage) - Ranks surviving mutants by whether a killing test is worth writing, using batched TypeSafe Jev judgments (`jtt`); hooks for pre-commit and GitHub Actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-test-triage.md).
- [jev-ticket-triage](https://github.com/beese54/jev-ticket-triage) - Reproducible support-ticket triage eval: TypeSafe Jev vs Together.ai LLMs on accuracy/cost/latency/confidence (WIP). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/beese54-jev-ticket-triage.md).
- [jev-tool-search](https://github.com/kachar/jev-tool-search) - Benchmark and experimental engine comparing BM25, embeddings, rerankers, and TypeSafe Jev for selecting among 525 real MCP tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-tool-search.md).
- [jev-toolkit](https://github.com/jbt95/jev-toolkit) - MCP-first TypeSafe/Jev toolkit: `jev mcp` plus CLI triage/audit/label/route and optional Prometheus impact metrics. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-toolkit.md).
- [jev-tree](https://github.com/reachjalil/jev-tree) - Recursive TypeSafe Jev Choice over a JSON taxonomy so catalogs can exceed the 255-option flat Choice cap. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-tree.md).
- [jev-triage](https://github.com/CMaintz/jev-triage) - GitHub Action that labels issues with TypeSafe or Cloudflare Jev typed questions and confidence-gated applies. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-triage.md).
- [jev-triage (ZephyrDeng)](https://github.com/ZephyrDeng/jev-triage) - Triage a GitHub or GitLab backlog with TypeSafe Jev: duplicates, related topics, issue types and dependency order in one HTML report (gh/glab). Distinct from other jev-triage listings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/zephyrdeng-jev-triage.md).
- [jev-ultrafast-skill (SherseaHe)](https://github.com/SherseaHe/jev-ultrafast-skill) - Portable agent Skill wrapping browser-use/jev-ultrafast for Codex-style agents: bounded Chrome runs with result verification. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sherseahe-jev-ultrafast-skill.md).
- [jev-use (shitianfang)](https://github.com/shitianfang/jev-use) - Hands the Claude Code, Codex and pi steps that need no text output to Jev, returning anything it should not decide to the LLM under a typed escalation contract. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-use.md).
- [jev-verify (stillmarcus24)](https://github.com/stillmarcus24/jev-verify) - Checks published Jev outputs against the Yurin confidence identity (distinct from dsh-jev-verify). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stillmarcus-jev-verify.md).
- [jev-web](https://github.com/warsang/jev-web) - Browser runtime for open-weight typed-decision models (open-jev/Laya/Strands/…); local after weight cache—independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-web.md).
- [jev-web-skills](https://github.com/jackson7705/jev-web-skills) - Five tested website-building workflows with TypeSafe Jev: page maps, content migration, silo and doorway QA, on-site routing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jackson7705-jev-web-skills.md).
- [JevBench (metamorphic)](https://github.com/JevBench/jevbench) - Metamorphic coherence benchmark for typed probabilistic decision models (50 probability/choice laws; no gold labels). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevbench.md).
- [JevDo](https://github.com/yinhong-zhou/jevdo) - Jev-first DeepSeek Harness agent loop that reuses validated Actions before calling a frontier model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdo.md).
- [jevex-cli](https://github.com/trolloks/jevex-cli) - Jev-powered per-turn Codex model router with a terminal UI (jevex). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevex-cli.md).
- [JevGate (RichieLoco)](https://github.com/RichieLoco/JevGate) - Pure-MQL5 MetaTrader 5 module that vetoes EA trades using TypeSafe Jev calibrated judgments (distinct from Claude Code jevgate plugins). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/richieloco-jevgate.md).
- [jevmate](https://github.com/GastonGelhorn/jevmate) - Shell/CLI, Claude Code plugin, and MCP server for coding-agent triage, test selection, and risk review via TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmate.md).
- [JevOss](https://github.com/mertkayacs/jevoss) - Probe suite for any Jev-API decision model: accuracy, calibration, and adversarial recipes (Jev/Laya/etc.). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevoss.md).
- [JevSceneMiner](https://github.com/bskkimm/JevSceneMiner) - Mines driving maneuvers from Autoware rosbags and nuPlan logs with TypeSafe Jev: motion, lane geometry and object interactions become a readable script, Jev classifies lateral and longitudinal labels with probabilities, and answers merge into timestamped scenes with a camera/BEV/timeline review viewer and ground-truth editor. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-scene-miner.md).
- [jevtri (Jev Triage)](https://github.com/takeshiue/jevtri) - Go CLI for the first minutes of a Linux server incident: collects log lines around the incident time, masks secrets, and asks TypeSafe Jev how worth investigating each log is, so you know which log to read first; signed rpm/deb releases and English/Japanese/Chinese docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtri.md).
- [jevwright](https://github.com/Ice-Hazymoon/jevwright) - Browser E2E tests written as user steps: TypeSafe Jev selects controls once; recordings replay without the model; application code owns pass/fail. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevwright.md).
- [JevX (vij-sameerb5)](https://github.com/vij-sameerb5/JevX) - Find hardcoded judgment calls in a codebase and replace strong fits with TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score (tests as proof). Distinct from hawkyre/jevx and muthuishere/jevx. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/vij-sameerb5-jevx.md).
- [jev-zork](https://github.com/Resadan-dev/jev-zork) - TypeSafe Jev plays Zork I: Choice over Jericho valid actions with anti-loop policy and a French replay dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-zork.md).
- [jev.zig](https://github.com/jakeknowlton/jev.zig) - Unofficial Zig client for the TypeSafe System One API (Jev): compile-time typed noul/choice/score questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jakeknowlton-jev-zig.md).
- [jev2mcp](https://github.com/n8mirai/jev2mcp) - Local companion + Chrome extension: TypeSafe Jev picks ChatGPT MCP/plugin/tool mentions from a configured catalog. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev2mcp.md).
- [jev4j](https://github.com/maxsumrall/jev4j) - Java 17+ TypeSafe/OpenRouter Jev library (Maven Central) with enum choice/score helpers and Spring Boot starter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev4j.md).
- [jev4k](https://github.com/pambrose/jev4k) - Kotlin DSL/client for TypeSafe Jev Noul/Choice/Score (Maven Central `com.pambrose:jev4k`; distinct from jev-java / jev-android). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev4k.md).
- [Jev_validation_agent](https://github.com/omkarchougule19/Jev_validation_agent) - Python Jev Guard: TypeSafe Jev validates agent outputs with checks, reports, and a local demo UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-validation-agent.md).
- [jeval](https://github.com/rlaope/jeval) - Measures probabilistic classifier calibration (incl. Jev confidence) and cost-optimal human hand-off thresholds; offline demo included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeval.md).
- [jevals](https://github.com/dayhaysoos/jevals) - Local TypeSafe Jev evaluation workbench: author Noul/Choice/Score cases with expected answers, run them, and compare saved results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevals.md).
- [JEVals (duberblock)](https://github.com/duberblock/JEVals) - Open-source playground for System One / JEV-compatible evals: fidelity, semantic judging, and independent verification across web screens (distinct from dayhaysoos/jevals and Jevals.com). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/duberblock-jevals.md).
- [Jevals.com](https://jevals.com/) - Hosted independent boards: TypeSafe Jev vs LLMs on PubMedQA/Banking77/HelpSteer2 (calibration/cost/latency); data CC BY 4.0 (distinct from dayhaysoos/jevals). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevals-com.md).
- [Jevaluate](https://github.com/ElshinQ/jevaluate) - Confidence-gated web-app walkthroughs with TypeSafe Jev (optional DeepSeek vision) plus eval/judge scripts and an agent skill. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevaluate.md).
- [JevAny](https://github.com/SimpleJev/JevAny) - Open infrastructure for training, evaluating, and deploying System 1 decision models (independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevany.md).
- [JevApi](https://github.com/JawzoD3TH/JevApi) - .NET 10 TypeSafe Jev client (AOT) plus stdio MCP evaluate/batch tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevapi.md).
- [jevbench (dhruvmehra)](https://github.com/dhruvmehra/jevbench) - Reproduce TypeSafe Jev vs LLM/BERT/Laya/NLI text-classification accuracy, calibration, latency, throughput, and cost on public datasets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dhruvmehra-jevbench.md).
- [jevbrief](https://github.com/Parthkomalwad/jevbrief) - Playwright briefing for TypeSafe Jev: filter page elements with drop reasons, Choice next-click, local trace viewer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevbrief.md).
- [jevbus](https://github.com/zkjoie/jevbus) - Rust streaming event bus: TypeSafe Jev (or any Judge) decides route/subscribe/deliver dispositions with policy thresholds. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevbus.md).
- [jevc](https://github.com/doronp/jevc) - Compiles natural-language agent rules and JSON Schemas into TypeSafe Jev programs (typed questions + code reducers) you can test offline. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevc.md).
- [jevcache](https://github.com/kushals256/jevcache) - OpenAI-compatible local cache proxy: TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter Decisions) admits same-intent paraphrases so expensive chat completions can be skipped (fail-open on Jev errors). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcache.md).
- [jevci (sumant1122)](https://github.com/sumant1122/jevci) - Sub-second CI/diff/commit/doc quality gate powered by TypeSafe Jev System One (score+noul). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sumant1122-jevci.md).
- [JevClient.jl](https://github.com/AtelierArith/JevClient.jl) - Unofficial Julia client for TypeSafe System One (Noul/Choice/Score; endpoint policy to api.typesafe.ai). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevclient-jl.md).
- [jevcompat](https://github.com/mandu5/jevcompat) - Spec and conformance suite for Jev-compatible TypeSafe System One API servers (proxy, mock, GitHub Action). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcompat.md).
- [JevCore Agent](https://github.com/carter1111/jevcore) - Local coding harness (`jevcoreagent`): TypeSafe Jev task/risk/mode judgments plus hard-policy Guard, MCP + CLI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/carter1111-jevcore.md).
- [jevcut](https://github.com/VBS2004/jevcut) - Auto-clipper: code enumerates cut edges; TypeSafe Jev judges standalone clips for Shorts/Reels/TikTok (benchmarked on 38 videos). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcut.md).
- [jevdev](https://github.com/ibrahimcesar/jevdev) - Rust coding-agent harness built around TypeSafe Jev System One decisions (HTTP or local transport). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdev.md).
- [jevdevice](https://github.com/Xopher00/jevdevice) - MCP harness for Android (adb) or local shell: TypeSafe Jev (or local Laya) picks one runtime-discovered target per goal; code gates and executes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdevice.md).
- [jeveloper](https://github.com/pur4v/jeveloper) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev reflex layer for route/gate/verify/completion and optional driver mode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeveloper.md).
- [JevEmbed](https://github.com/HITsz-TMG/JevEmbed) - Embedding-based Choice/Score/Noul framework with Jev-shaped API/CLI/HTTP server; independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevembed.md).
- [JeVerifier](https://github.com/silvariasereneblossom/jeverifier) - Claude coding harness: TypeSafe Jev builds reading lists and contradiction/rule checks; Claude reviews. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeverifier.md).
- [jevernetes](https://github.com/sunil-sadasivan/jevernetes) - Live Kubernetes log analysis (CLI/dashboard) with optional TypeSafe Jev judgments or offline keyword rules and agent handoff prompts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevernetes.md).
- [jevface](https://github.com/rbrisuda/jevface) - Typed judgments for Java: declare questions as an interface and let Jev answer them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevface.md).
- [jevfilter](https://github.com/damiensmith1/jevfilter) - Python library/PyPI: filter or classify text with plain-English rules via TypeSafe Jev (choose/check/rate). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/damiensmith1-jevfilter.md).
- [JevFlow](https://github.com/Parth1811/JevFlow) - Claude Code plugin that keeps agents honest: plans as phases with checks; when Claude tries to stop, JevFlow asks Jev if the work is really done. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/parth1811-jevflow.md).
- [jevframe](https://github.com/ktaletsk/jevframe) - Semantic AI for pandas and Polars: TypeSafe Jev `.jev` accessor returns structured answers with full probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevframe.md).
- [jevgate (craxrev)](https://github.com/craxrev/jevgate) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev facts gate Bash/Write allow/ask/deny (bypassPermissions-friendly). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/craxrev-jevgate.md).
- [jevgo](https://github.com/devbackend/jevgo) - Unofficial Go client for TypeSafe System One: typed Noul/Choice/Score in one call (stdlib HTTP). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevgo.md).
- [jevgrep (dzhng)](https://github.com/dzhng/jevgrep) - Coding-agent CLI (`jg` / `@dzhng/jevgrep`): ask what code does and get relevant files plus excerpts via Jev (TypeSafe, Vercel AI Gateway, or OpenRouter)—distinct from kyu1204/jgrep npm jevgrep. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dzhng-jevgrep.md).
- [JevGuard](https://github.com/Jhonnyr97/JevGuard) - Claude Code/Codex plugin: generate project rules from CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md and enforce them with TypeSafe Jev on PreToolUse/Stop (distinct from jev-guard risk scoring). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevguard.md).
- [JevGuard (blacksinisterx)](https://github.com/blacksinisterx/jev-guard) - Agent tool-execution security layer: hard rules then TypeSafe Jev allow/review/block (distinct from leepokai/jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/blacksinisterx-jev-guard.md).
- [jevkit](https://github.com/ariel-frischer/jevkit) - Rust CLI for TypeSafe Jev typed decisions plus offline `jev lint` before paid calls (distinct from Hermes's internal Python `jevkit` client). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevkit.md).
- [jevlang (RoyWiggins)](https://github.com/RoyWiggins/jevlang) - Python codec preprocessor: if/while/match conditions decided by TypeSafe Jev (including English conditions); upstream LICENSE file missing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/roywiggins-jevlang.md).
- [JevLang (TimMikeladze)](https://github.com/TimMikeladze/JevLang) - Policy engine for routes/gates/actions: Jev answers only policy questions; signed journal (distinct from sumanmichael/jevlang). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/timmikeladze-jevlang.md).
- [JevLangGraph (blacksinisterx)](https://github.com/blacksinisterx/jev-langgraph) - LangGraph agent where TypeSafe Jev picks continue/search/tool/retry/finish/escalate (distinct from other jev-langgraph owners). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/blacksinisterx-jev-langgraph.md).
- [jevlens](https://github.com/k4its1t/jevlens) - Evaluate, calibrate, replay, and monitor TypeSafe Jev decisions from labeled datasets with threshold suggestions and optional Streamlit/CI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlens.md).
- [Jevlet](https://github.com/NAME0x0/Jevlet) - Research reconstruction of a System One decision model (Noul/Choice/Score) plus a Windows command-palette daily driver; HF weights—independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlet.md).
- [Jevlike](https://github.com/vinnylarouge/jevlike) - Trains a small option-attention scorer with synthetic data and optional frozen encoders; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlike.md).
- [Jevlin](https://github.com/copyleftdev/jevlin) - Zig 0.16 SDK for TypeSafe Jev Choice/Score/Noul with bounded buffers, retries, and deadlines (independent of TypeSafe). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/copyleftdev-jevlin.md).
- [JevLint](https://github.com/huntedman/JevLint) - Semantic lint CLI: plain-English conventions scored as file-level TypeSafe Jev Noul judgments (`@jevlint/cli`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlint.md).
- [jevlint (Ice-Hazymoon)](https://github.com/Ice-Hazymoon/jevlint) - Semantic lint CLI (`@hazymoon/jevlint`): plain-English yes/no rules judged by TypeSafe Jev with calibrated probabilities (distinct from huntedman/JevLint and mizchi/jev-lint). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ice-hazymoon-jevlint.md).
- [jevmcp (eaisdevelopment)](https://github.com/eaisdevelopment/jevmcp) - Agent plugin + MCP: TypeSafe Jev screens spec-drift, CI triage, and code-audit checks for Claude Code/Codex. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/eaisdevelopment-jevmcp.md).
- [jevmem](https://github.com/Avinash-jetwani/jevmem) - Git-tracked JEVMEM.md project memory for Claude Code/Cursor/Codex; TypeSafe Jev decides what to save and recall, and checks tool calls against saved rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmem.md).
- [jevmetrics](https://github.com/ishantanu/jevmetrics) - Experimental OpenTelemetry Collector metrics processor: TypeSafe Jev assesses instrument metadata for retention; deterministic keep/reduce policy with caching (alpha). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmetrics.md).
- [jevmod](https://github.com/ohernandezdev/jevmod) - Moderation CLI/SDK/API/MCP and optional chat bots with per-category TypeSafe Jev probabilities and thresholds you own. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmod.md).
- [jevmory](https://github.com/romiluz13/jevmory) - Local coding-agent memory with verbatim quotes graded by TypeSafe Jev confidence and receipt-backed MEMORY.md audits. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmory.md).
- [jevnav](https://github.com/dtduc-git/jevnav) - Browser automation with TypeSafe Jev element choice, JSONL decision traces, risk gates, and offline CI replay (distinct from Ultrafast). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevnav.md).
- [jevonian](https://github.com/xinyao27/jevonian) - Local OpenAI/Anthropic/Responses proxy for coding agents where one Jev call answers both the model route and the thinking level for `jevonian/auto`, after code has filtered candidates by protocol, context window, effort floor, and spent quota windows; a pinned model or explicit `jevonian/<route>` skips Jev entirely, and costs in the ledger are estimates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevonian.md).
- [JevOnly](https://github.com/buluoray/JevOnly) - Pure-Jev browser agent: code enumerates page/goal options, Jev only picks, with verify/undo and an irreversible-action gate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevonly.md).
- [jevos](https://github.com/feder-cr/jev) - Self-hosted, Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` server answering yes/no (Noul) questions from a CPU-only 1B GGUF model (MIT); independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevos.md).
- [JevPaper](https://github.com/SRjoeee/jev-paper) - Chrome extension that uses TypeSafe Jev to highlight arXiv claim evidence and caveats on the authors' own sentences. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-paper.md).
- [jevper](https://github.com/zhulinchng/jevper) - Jev-shaped System One noul/choice/score client over any OpenAI-compatible model (no hosted TypeSafe API). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevper.md).
- [JevPilot](https://github.com/standardagents/jevpilot) - Demonstrates Jev maneuver selection in a browser driving simulation with local collision checks; application licensing is unspecified. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevpilot.md).
- [jevpilot (bloudhood)](https://github.com/bloudhood/jevpilot) - MCP browser that finishes goals with TypeSafe Jev picking each step (distinct from standardagents/jevpilot driving demo). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bloudhood-jevpilot.md).
- [JevPokerBench](https://github.com/Prophetlab/JevPokerBench) - Texas Hold'em benchmark/playground with official TypeSafe Jev, cash/SNG boards, and BYOK agents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-poker-bench.md).
- [jevq](https://github.com/who/jevq) - Jev filter sidecar for jq pipelines (`jq | jevq | jq`): keep JSONL values that pass a yes/no TypeSafe Jev question (or emit scores). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/who-jevq.md).
- [jevql](https://github.com/kylemclaren/jevql) - psql-style CLI, MCP server, and Go/TypeScript/Python SDKs that add TypeSafe Jev predicates, probabilities, choices, and scores to queries against vanilla PostgreSQL without an extension; distinct from pg-jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevql.md).
- [jevql (hemanth)](https://github.com/hemanth/jevql) - npm `jev-ql` calibrated semantic SQL over unstructured data with TypeSafe Jev (distinct from kylemclaren/jevql). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hemanth-jevql.md).
- [JevRepoTriage](https://github.com/murongg/JevRepoTriage) - Self-hosted GitHub issue/PR triage assistant powered by TypeSafe Jev (web UI + workers). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevrepo-triage.md).
- [Jevris](https://github.com/CryptVenture/Jevris) - Local decide/verify/route control plane for coding agents using TypeSafe System One Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevris.md).
- [JevRoute (suncirkles)](https://github.com/suncirkles/jev-router) - Decision-only coding-task model routing (JevRoute) with a separate evaluation harness and recorded experiment evidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/suncirkles-jev-router.md).
- [JevRouter](https://github.com/BillionsBobby/JevRouter) - Capability router: TypeSafe Jev (or OpenRouter Decisions) picks among models/subagents/skills/MCP/CLIs while code enforces permissions, risk, confirmation, and receipts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevrouter.md).
- [Jevs](https://github.com/nshcr/jevs) - Bun/TypeScript MCP server and Codex plugin for TypeSafe Jev classify/score/check/batch via the official JS SDK. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevs.md).
- [JevScope](https://github.com/jeiel85/jevscope) - Local-first visual workbench and JSONL regression testbench for TypeSafe Jev projects. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevscope.md).
- [jevsearch](https://github.com/kylemclaren/jevsearch) - shadcn/ui registry block for ⌘K site search that shows keyword hits immediately, then re-ranks the top candidates with one TypeSafe Jev request; no embeddings or vector database. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevsearch.md).
- [jevseek](https://github.com/blingdivinity/jevseek) - DeepSeek proposes next tokens; TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter System One chooses which to append. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevseek.md).
- [jevsh](https://github.com/takeshiue/jevsh) - Bash wrapper: TypeSafe Jev scores shell command risk (LOW–CRITICAL) before you confirm run. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevsh.md).
- [JevShield](https://github.com/lgy1027/jevshield) - Python/LangChain tool-call security gate using TypeSafe Jev Choice/Noul/Score with local heuristic fallback (distinct from Node jev-shield). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevshield.md).
- [jevskillz](https://github.com/calelamb/jevskillz) - Claude Code skills + `jev` CLI for calibrated TypeSafe Jev checks over claims, tests, acceptance, and triage evidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevskillz.md).
- [JevSQL](https://github.com/EugeneBoondock/jevsql) - Adds TypeSafe Jev natural-language predicates and decision tables to SQLite SQL with batching, caching, and review queues; distinct from pg-jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevsql.md).
- [JevT++](https://github.com/wiatrM/jevtpp) - C++20 library for typed System One–style decisions (enums/booleans/scores) with optional local Laya (ONNX/ggml) or remote backends—independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtpp.md).
- [JevTape](https://github.com/Hugo-DDT/JevTape) - Record/replay/inspect TypeSafe Jev decisions as JSON cassettes with Decision Contract fingerprints for offline CI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtape.md).
- [jevtok](https://github.com/LabGuy94/jevtok) - Exact token counting and request-cost prediction for TypeSafe Jev (tiktoken-style encoder reconstructed from API usage). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtok.md).
- [jevtok-ts](https://github.com/aarzhaev/jevtok-ts) - Offline Jev token counting and request accounting for Node.js/TypeScript (companion to LabGuy94/jevtok). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtok-ts.md).
- [JevTrace](https://github.com/123wwwa/JevTrace) - MCP retrieval filter: TypeSafe Jev keeps only relevant snippets to cut agent tokens. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtrace.md).
- [JevTree (Chuf-H)](https://github.com/Chuf-H/jev-tree) - Probability tree/graph runtime over TypeSafe Jev local action distributions for verifiable multi-step decisions (distinct from reachjalil/jev-tree). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/chuf-h-jev-tree.md).
- [jevtriage](https://github.com/sathariels/jevtriage) - GitHub Action + CLI: triage PRs with TypeSafe Jev (`ready` / `needs_review` / `risky`) and confidence-gated exits. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtriage.md).
- [jevtrim](https://github.com/pdrpinto/jevtrim) - Benchmarks TypeSafe Jev as a context-relevance judge vs retrieval on LoCoMo with matched token budgets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtrim.md).
- [jevx (muthuishere)](https://github.com/muthuishere/jevx) - Agent skill + CLI for typed yes/no/choice/rating gut checks via System One (hosted Jev or self-hosted); distinct from hawkyre/jevx browser extension. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/muthuishere-jevx.md).
- [jevyoumean](https://github.com/syumai/jevyoumean) - Semantic CLI "Did you mean?": TypeSafe Jev matches unknown subcommands by intent from help text (`jym`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevyoumean.md).
- [jevzf](https://github.com/zachlandes/jevzf) - Meaning search for fzf: rank piped lines by TypeSafe Jev relevance (CLI filter or stock fzf Ctrl-R binding); unofficial. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevzf.md).
- [jgrep](https://github.com/keltokhy/jgrep) - Filters text, structured records, functions, and diff hunks against plain-English descriptions using Jev Noul judgments. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jgrep.md).
- [jgrep (npm: jevgrep)](https://github.com/kyu1204/jgrep) - Node CLI that gates a diff in CI on a rule written in English (`--diff`, grep exit codes) and lists the test files a diff can affect (`--tests`), one TypeSafe Jev Noul per code chunk, diff hunk, or CSV row; distinct from the Python jgrep. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jgrep-jevgrep.md).
- [JIS · PARALLELIZE](https://github.com/lovstudio/jis) - Agent swarm where rules do zero-token coordination and TypeSafe Jev judges verify/adopt/dispute when rules cannot. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jis-parallelize.md).
- [JIT-JEV Context OS](https://github.com/wojciechwiesner/jit-context) - Epistemic context runtime + TypeSafe Jev System 1 gate for tool-using coding agents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jit-context.md).
- [jlink](https://github.com/keltokhy/jlink) - Links records under a plain-English match rule using Jev Noul pair judgments, with local candidate blocking and match resolution. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jlink.md).
- [jmc-jev-mcp](https://github.com/thegreystone/jmc-jev-mcp) - MCP server that judges JDK Flight Recorder recordings with TypeSafe Jev typed questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jmc-jev-mcp.md).
- [JMP](https://github.com/morcoan/JMP) - Local coding workspace where TypeSafe Jev selects the next tool action and DeepSeek/Codex/Bonsai supply arguments for OpenHands/MCP execution. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jmp.md).
- [jselect](https://github.com/keltokhy/jselect) - Selects source-linked evidence within a token budget using Jev Noul relevance judgments and local diversity-aware selection. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jselect.md).
- [jsort](https://github.com/keltokhy/jsort) - Semantic sort CLI: order lines along a plain-English dimension via pairwise TypeSafe Jev comparisons and a Bradley–Terry scale. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jsort.md).
- [Juardrails](https://github.com/abhaybhargav/juardrails) - Go guardrails service for TypeSafe Jev policies (YAML/UI, namespaces, audit, REST/CLI); repository license unspecified at review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/juardrails.md).
- [judge_rails](https://github.com/just-the-v/judge_rails) - Rails gem that stores TypeSafe Jev Noul/Choice/Score answers as ordinary, indexable ActiveRecord attributes kept up to date, with SQL scopes such as `urgency_above(0.8)`; also a plain Ruby client. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/judge-rails.md).
- [judgment (Rust)](https://github.com/chussenot/judgment) - Rust crate that turns TypeSafe System One (Jev) answers into verified, testable Rust types — typed choice/noul/score questions, a `Fake` backend that checks scripted answers like a server would, and `.jud` rubric documents that keep thresholds beside the questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/judgment.md).
- [jurl](https://github.com/rayoplateado/jurl) - curl that reads the page for you: `jurl -q "question" <url>` fetches a page (with a headless browser when it needs JavaScript), and TypeSafe Jev picks the passages, links or code that answer, with Cloudflare Clef-flash for images; no chatbot, prose stays verbatim. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jurl.md).
- [Kafka Jev Connector](https://github.com/smatiolids/kafka-jev-connector) - Kafka Connect sink connector that sends each record from your input topics to TypeSafe Jev with a question set you configure and publishes an enriched record (original message, answers, trace and dedupe IDs) to an output topic; at-least-once delivery, dead-letter topic, Confluent Cloud custom-connector or self-managed Connect. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kafka-jev-connector.md).
- [Kassad](https://github.com/jacob-berendsohn/kassad) - .NET LLM guardrails: TypeSafe Jev typed checks return Allow/Flag/Review/Block with calibrated confidence (ASP.NET middleware). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kassad.md).
- [Kestra TypeSafe plugin](https://github.com/kestra-io/plugin-typesafe) - Kestra plugin for TypeSafe System One typed evaluations inside orchestrated workflows (not Scala Typesafe). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kestra-plugin-typesafe.md).
- [kevala](https://github.com/bvolpato/kevala) - Zero-dependency Rust→WASM decision models (Laya/Kev/…) in any web page with WebGPU; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kevala.md).
- [KiroGraph](https://github.com/davide-desio-eleva/kirograph) - Local semantic code knowledge graph and MCP server for Kiro (experimental support for other agents) whose opt-in memory-relation, wiki-contradiction and auth-detection modes can use TypeSafe Jev typed classification. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kirograph.md).
- [Klassify](https://github.com/fajarnuha/klassify) - Kotlin Multiplatform DSL/SDK and Native CLI/MCP for TypeSafe System One Noul/Choice/Score (distinct from jev4k). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/klassify.md).
- [L2S1](https://github.com/LuticaCANARD/L2S1) - Local GGUF chat-model → typed binary/choice/ordinal decisions (Rust CLI + Python/TS/C++ SDKs); independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/l2s1.md).
- [langgraph-jev](https://github.com/iroy2000/langgraph-jev) - LangGraph/LangChain integration for TypeSafe Jev decision API (distinct from blacksinisterx/jev-langgraph). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/langgraph-jev.md).
- [Laravel AI](https://github.com/laravel/ai) - Provides typed classification and a TypeSafe provider for Laravel applications, with fake responses for application testing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laravel-ai.md).
- [Laya Spring Boot Starter](https://github.com/kelsonthony/laya-springboot-starter) - Community Spring Boot 4 starter that auto-configures a `LayaClient` (Spring MVC + RestClient + Jackson 3) for self-hosted Laya's Jev-compatible `POST /v1/systemone`, with typed `choice`, `score` and `noul` questions and several decisions in one request; inspired by the Jev Spring Boot Starter but an original implementation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-springboot-starter.md).
- [laya-candle](https://github.com/Trystan-SA/laya-candle) - Pure-Rust Candle port of Laya for local typed Choice/Score/Noul decisions (no Python; independent of hosted Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-candle.md).
- [Laya for Home Assistant](https://github.com/allenporter/home-assistant-laya) - Fully local Home Assistant Assist conversation agent using open-weight Laya (sibling architecture to TypeSafe Jev HA; independent of hosted Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/home-assistant-laya.md).
- [laya-compaction](https://github.com/bussolabs/laya-compaction) - Port of the listed fast-jev-compaction that swaps TypeSafe Jev for Laya, the open Jev-compatible decision model: a Claude Code plugin and npm library that replaces the compaction summary with per-item decisions — every tool call and result is scored, stale ones are dropped or truncated, and everything kept (including all user and assistant text) stays verbatim; Laya runs locally via a shared `laya-serve` or on your own server through `LAYA_URL`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-compaction.md).
- [laya-router](https://github.com/bussolabs/laya-router) - Port of gargpratyush/jev-router (listed) that takes per-turn model routing decisions from Laya — the open, Jev-compatible System One model — instead of TypeSafe Jev: `laya-claude` and `laya-codex` launch the real Claude Code / Codex CLIs behind a local proxy that sends simple turns to the fast tier and hard ones to the strong tier, running Laya locally with no API key or against your own `LAYA_URL` server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-router.md).
- [layajev](https://github.com/metalagman/layajev) - Local Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` server running verified Laya in-process (Go/ONNX; subset API). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/layajev.md).
- [laya-php](https://github.com/marcreichel/laya-php) - PHP/Laravel SDK for local Laya typed Choice/Score/Noul decisions (Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` alternative; self-hosted). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-php.md).
- [laya-skill](https://github.com/StevenLi-phoenix/laya-skill) - Claude Code skill/plugin for System One: local Laya (incl. WebGPU) or hosted TypeSafe Jev, plus fine-tune helpers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-skill.md).
- [laya-guardrails](https://github.com/javimp2003/laya-guardrails) - Self-hosted Laya System One guardrails (input/tool/output) for ES/PT agents—typed probabilities, deterministic policy; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-guardrails.md).
- [laya-jev-GraphRAG](https://github.com/bodepudimuneendra-netizen/laya-jev-GraphRAG) - Graph-DB-agnostic Agentic GraphRAG pipeline that uses swappable System One models (local Laya / cloud Jev) for Score/Noul/Choice across ingestion, traversal, and post-retrieval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-jev-graphrag.md).
- [laya-packet-analyser](https://github.com/esterhuizen/laya-packet-analyser) - Stdlib Python packet security analyser: deterministic detectors plus local Laya (Jev-compatible /v1/systemone) triage and live dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-packet-analyser.md).
- [laya-vs-jev](https://github.com/zaferayan/laya-vs-jev) - Head-to-head local Laya (MLX) / Ollaya vs TypeSafe Jev on ~900 cases across 3 tasks and 6 languages. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/zaferayan-laya-vs-jev.md).
- [ldraw-nova](https://github.com/anteloc/ldraw-nova) - Agent tooling that turns a model idea into an LDraw LEGO model; part and example-model search is re-ranked by TypeSafe Jev through jev-rerank (AGPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ldraw-nova.md).
- [Leash](https://github.com/CMaintz/leash) - Jev-powered coding-agent guardrail that judges each turn against un-lintable project rules with a one-way accepted-debt ratchet. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/leash.md).
- [lev (Abhinavexists)](https://github.com/Abhinavexists/lev) - Open Qwen3.5-4B LoRA System One decisions (Choice/Noul/Score) over `/v1/systemone`; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lev.md).
- [libsemop](https://github.com/jasonduncan/libsemop) - C++17 port of semantic-operators built on libtypesafe: write a typed semantic judgment once (Boolean, Choice, Score, enum), run it on TypeSafe Jev or another System One provider, and get "don't know" instead of a guess below a confidence threshold; filtering, reranking, cascades and benchmarks included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/libsemop.md).
- [Lichen](https://github.com/Mushroom-Systems/lichen) - Local API-compatible Jev/System One replacement (choice/noul/score + images) on open weights—independent of TypeSafe-hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lichen.md).
- [lintent](https://github.com/pietervp/lintent) - Plain-language lint rules judged by TypeSafe Jev, scoped with tree-sitter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lintent.md).
- [llama-index-jev](https://github.com/WiktorB2004/llama-index-jev) - Python integrations for LlamaIndex passage reranking and query-engine selection, with configurable error and selection behavior. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llama-index-jev.md).
- [llm-typesafe](https://github.com/simonw/llm-typesafe) - Simon Willison LLM CLI plugin for TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score (`jev` / `typesafe/jev-latest`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llm-typesafe.md).
- [llmbridge](https://github.com/dragonlin-ai/llmbridge) - OpenAI-compatible LLM routing gateway with TypeSafe Jev as the L2 decision layer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llmbridge.md).
- [LLM vs Jev](https://github.com/codergarten/llmvsjev) - Teaching demo that races Gemini against TypeSafe Jev on the same 1,000 bank transactions into 12 categories, side by side, scored on speed and accuracy, with a no-key mock mode and a tiny Node proxy for the Jev key. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llmvsjev.md).
- [Lynkgrids AI for Jev](https://github.com/lynkread/Lynkgrids-AI-for-Jev) - Question pack, CLI, agent skill and Claude Code plugin that make typed TypeSafe Jev decisions on a Lynkgrids LinkedIn-leads pipeline — lead fit, reply intent, draft check and meeting readiness — each one Jev request with an `act` / `review` / `human` route set by confidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lynkgrids-ai-for-jev.md).
- [macos-computer-use-kit](https://github.com/Sur-Cai/macos-computer-use-kit) - AX-first macOS computer-use MCP/CLI with optional TypeSafe Jev semantic guards before irreversible actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/macos-computer-use-kit.md).
- [MakerAi](https://github.com/gustavoeenriquez/MakerAi) - Native Delphi AI framework (agents, RAG, MCP, multi-provider chat) with `TAiJev` and eight Jev adapters for routing, tool/input guardrails, RAG reranking, eval scoring, bulk labeling and model routing — hosted Jev or Ollama `/v1/systemone` decision models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/makerai.md).
- [Malkuth](https://github.com/newfull5/malkuth) - Open-weight multilingual System One–style decision models (Korean focus) served via Kev `/v1/systemone`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/malkuth.md).
- [mayi](https://github.com/AidenHadisi/mayi) - Permission gate for Claude Code/Cursor/Codex: TypeSafe Jev scores tool calls; dialog on unsafe (errors deny). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mayi.md).
- [mcp-agent-openjev](https://github.com/ChristofMilius/mcp-agent-openjev) - Local OpenJev Choice/Noul/Score decision service for agents via MCP + CLI (LM Studio logprobs; not TypeSafe-hosted). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/christofmilius-mcp-agent-openjev.md).
- [Mechanical Jev](https://github.com/Lasimeri/Mechanical-Jev) - Rust client/`mjev` CLI/eval harness for System One Noul/Choice/Score against local Intel Phi Jev or compatible `/v1/systemone`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mechanical-jev.md).
- [memsearch](https://github.com/zilliztech/memsearch) - Persistent, Markdown-backed memory layer for Claude Code, Codex, DeepSeek Harness, OpenClaw and OpenCode (Milvus index), with optional TypeSafe Jev reranking that scores every recalled candidate in one `/v1/systemone` request; off by default and enabled only in global config. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/memsearch.md).
- [metajev](https://github.com/YYTbit/metajev) - Cache the decision, compute the policy: record System One distributions under state/question/model keys; change thresholds without re-running Jev (MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/metajev.md).
- [Metis](https://github.com/Ayush0054/metis) - GitHub Action and Python CLI that triage new issues with TypeSafe Jev category labels and missing-detail follow-ups. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/metis.md).
- [Micdrop](https://github.com/Godefroy/micdrop) - TypeScript voice conversation SDK (browser/RN/Node) with official `@micdrop/typesafe` Jev turn classifier (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/micdrop.md).
- [Midscene JEV Runner](https://github.com/KiritoKing/midscene-jev-runner) - Community Midscene/Playwright runner: TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter Decisions) chooses browser ops on a caller-owned page. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/midscene-jev-runner.md).
- [Milvus Model](https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus-model) - Python reranker adapter that sends candidate documents as Jev Noul questions in one request, then sorts the returned scores and preserves original document indices. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/milvus-model.md).
- [Ministry of Truth](https://github.com/LukasPodhorny/ministry-of-truth) - Spin-doctor web game: over a 15-day campaign you write cover stories in free text and five literal-minded Jev factions score every word — affect, credibility, salience, compliance, narrative fit — fully playable offline with a mock ensemble or live through a local key-holding proxy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ministry-of-truth.md).
- [Misogi](https://github.com/nadsous/Misogi) - Second-opinion sidecar for Claude Code/Codex/Kimi: TypeSafe Jev judges each "done" claim before you accept it. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/misogi.md).
- [MM3](https://github.com/mvp-scale/mm3) - Memory and decision layer for coding agents (Claude Code plugin, npm `@mvpscale/mm3`, CLI) that wraps TypeSafe Jev: the agent asks batches of yes/no and decision questions about a scoped piece of code and gets calibrated answers recorded in a ledger. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mm3.md).
- [mnemon-memory-agent](https://github.com/Grivn/mnemon-memory-agent) - Agent long-term memory judged with TypeSafe Jev System One over raw records. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mnemon-memory-agent.md).
- [mobai-ci](https://github.com/MobAI-App/mobai-ci) - Run MobAI `.mob` / Maestro mobile UI flows in CI; `.mobflow` steps are judged/acted by TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mobai-ci.md).
- [Mobile Jev](https://github.com/droidrun/mobile-jev) - Selects Android actions and exact input text through Mobilerun, with a local studio and task-specific verification demo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mobile-jev.md).
- [model-router (Claude Code)](https://github.com/Akurganow/model-router) - Claude Code mod that asks TypeSafe Jev one Score question about each redacted subagent task before it starts and sets the subagent model to haiku, sonnet or opus; `suggest` mode only logs, and the hook runs no commands and writes no files. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/akurganow-model-router.md).
- [model-router (muhx)](https://github.com/muhx/model-router) - Claude Code mod that picks the model and reasoning effort for each task with Jev — subagent model at spawn, main-loop effort per turn, and (on by default in this fork) main-loop model — using TypeSafe's `/v1/systemone` (`jev-latest`) or the Vercel AI Gateway, with a risky-task `noul`, confidence-aware policy and a built-in fallback classifier. A derivative of claude-code-templates' `jev-model-router` (also listed) under MIT. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/muhx-model-router.md).
- [model-router-python](https://github.com/TheCoder30ec4/model_router_python) - Zero-dependency Python router: hard limit filters, then TypeSafe Jev chooses the cheapest capable model id. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/model-router-python.md).
- [ModelRudder](https://github.com/cybrking/modelrudder) - Free local community preview that adds model routing to the native Codex terminal: your own TypeSafe Jev key classifies each request so the strongest model is spent only where needed, while Codex keeps its tools, approvals and workspace permissions; observe, auto and pinned modes, checksum-verified release installer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/modelrudder.md).
- [Moongate](https://github.com/brickfrog/moongate) - GitHub Action that evaluates committed diffs against JSON semantic rules with TypeSafe Jev and reports annotations from your thresholds. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/moongate.md).
- [Moreno.Jev](https://github.com/MorenoLand/Moreno.Jev) - Cross-platform MCP server and agent skill for TypeSafe Jev structured code review and debugging (review_code / review_diff tools). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/morenoland-moreno-jev.md).
- [Movo](https://github.com/truehannan/movo) - Linux (Debian-family) desktop computer-use agent: AT-SPI observation, typed TypeSafe Jev decisions, floating PySide6 panel. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/movo.md).
- [mu](https://github.com/qybaihe/mu) - Early-stage coding agent built on pi (CLI + desktop app) with a judgment kernel: Jev answers typed questions at decision points such as tool approval, constraint checks, context admission and test-log trimming, each switchable active/shadow/off and logged to a verdict ledger. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mu.md).
- [Mule 4 TypeSafe Connector](https://github.com/MuleSoft-Forge/mule4-typesafe-connector) - Mule 4 connector for TypeSafe System One / Jev typed noul/choice/score answers that drive Mule flows. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mule4-typesafe-connector.md).
- [multi-modal-jev-browser](https://github.com/foklepoint/multi-modal-jev-browser) - Browser agent (CLI `mmjb`, Python library and 13-tool MCP server) where a decision model picks each step — Jev via OpenRouter reading the element list, or Clef reading a numbered screenshot — and a vision LLM takes over only when the decision model is unsure, claims nothing can move, or the page stops changing; writes are off by default. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/multi-modal-jev-browser.md).
- [mushy-lint](https://github.com/napisani/mushy-lint) - Semantic code linter that extracts complete constructs with tree-sitter, asks Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter) narrow yes/no questions about them, and reports the raw probability of yes with the source evidence — for conventions that depend on meaning rather than syntax. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mushy-lint.md).
- [mysql-ailike](https://github.com/maayanlevy/mysql-ailike) - MySQL native `AILIKE` / `ailike` UDFs for natural-language row filters and joins via TypeSafe Jev (Linux plugin; GPL-2.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mysql-ailike.md).
- [n8n-nodes-jev](https://github.com/vibe-with-me-tools/n8n-nodes-jev) - n8n community node for TypeSafe Jev: classify, route, and score workflow text (distinct from khmuhtadin and @typesafe-ai packages). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-jev.md).
- [n8n-nodes-system-one](https://github.com/diegohh0411/n8n-nodes-system-one) - n8n community node for System One decision models across providers — TypeSafe Jev directly, Jev via OpenRouter (no TypeSafe account needed), Cloudflare Workers AI Clef, or any custom `/v1/systemone` endpoint — with typed questions and flat probability outputs for IF/Switch nodes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-system-one.md).
- [n8n-nodes-typesafe](https://github.com/Biztactix/n8n-nodes-typesafe) - n8n community node for TypeSafe System One noul/choice/score questions over workflow text or JSON. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-typesafe.md).
- [n8n-nodes-typesafe-ai](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/n8n-nodes-typesafe-ai) - Official TypeSafe AI n8n community nodes for System One Evaluate/Route (Jev and other System One models); distinct from Biztactix/khmuhtadin community packages. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-typesafe-ai.md).
- [NanoJev](https://github.com/TianyuCodings/NanoJev) - Studies independent Qwen-based typed decision heads, local serving, and game controllers with recorded comparisons. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nanojev.md).
- [naturalcodz](https://github.com/SuparvaCode/naturalcodz) - npm natural-logic helpers (`is`/`pick`/`rate`/`classify`/guards) powered by TypeSafe Jev with confidence thresholds. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/naturalcodz.md).
- [Navigator (qf-studio)](https://github.com/qf-studio/navigator) - Context-engineering plugin for Claude Code (loop mode, research, memories, live `/nav` pane) whose keyword prompt gates can get an optional typed TypeSafe Jev judge — one request per prompt, decisive answers override, the rest fall back to keywords. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/qf-studio-navigator.md).
- [neo4jev](https://github.com/jexp/neo4jev) - Explores Neo4j paths using next-hop choices and goal judgments, with notebooks and a Streamlit interface. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/neo4jev.md).
- [NeuroLink](https://github.com/juspay/neurolink) - TypeScript multi-provider AI SDK with a `decide`/`tryDecide` inference type powered by TypeSafe Jev for routing and gating. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/neurolink.md).
- [nf-jev](https://github.com/nextflow-io/nf-jev) - Nextflow plugin exposing TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score as pipeline functions (beta). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nf-jev.md).
- [nifi-jev](https://github.com/gbesse/nifi-jev) - Apache NiFi RouteWithJev processor: TypeSafe Jev semantic yes/no routing with review and failure lanes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nifi-jev.md).
- [Noma](https://github.com/blackdromeai-labs/noma) - Noma (Blackdrome AI Labs): open-weight MPL-2.0 System One decision model served by `pip install blackdrome-noma` / `noma serve` on the same `/v1/systemone` API as Jev, returning per-option probabilities plus abstain and uncertainty signals, with a paper on Zenodo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/noma.md).
- [Note Filer](https://github.com/someka-vrc/obsidian-note-filer) - Obsidian plugin that classifies notes with TypeSafe Jev and moves them into Thema/IAB taxonomy folders after review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/obsidian-note-filer.md).
- [NucleiSniper](https://github.com/MorDavid/NucleiSniper) - Security-testing pipeline that fingerprints each authorised target and has TypeSafe Jev score every Nuclei template's relevance (0–4) in batches, then runs Nuclei with all templates ordered highest-relevance first, so likely findings surface early; scans are resumable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nucleisniper.md).
- [Octolib](https://github.com/Muvon/octolib) - One Rust API for 30+ AI providers (chat, tools, structured output, embeddings, reranking, media) whose `evaluation` feature asks TypeSafe Jev typed noul/choice/score questions — directly (`typesafe:jev-latest`) or billed through Cloudflare AI Gateway (`cloudflare:typesafe/jev`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/octolib.md).
- [oh-my-jev (apetcu)](https://github.com/apetcu/oh-my-jev) - oh-my-pi plugin: TypeSafe Jev tool-call gate (default), optional model router, and latency telemetry — distinct from MassiveLabsNet/oh-my-jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apetcu-oh-my-jev.md).
- [olla-jev](https://github.com/nvkudva/olla-jev) - Ollama-style local CLI/server for Hugging Face System One models behind the Jev /v1/systemone API. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/olla-jev.md).
- [Ollaya](https://github.com/ollaya-dev/ollaya) - 'Ollama for decision models': one Rust binary that pulls open decision models by name (Laya, winnow, decider, NLI, GLiClass), serves them from a local daemon and speaks TypeSafe's `/v1/systemone`, `/v1/decisions` and `/v1/models` wire format, so existing Jev clients and the official SDK work by pointing `TYPESAFE_BASE_URL` at localhost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ollaya.md).
- [omo-jev-plugin](https://github.com/brianhong-dev/omo-jev-plugin) - OmO / senpi plugin: TypeSafe Jev advises on skill fit, next tools, loops, and completion (shadow/advise/act modes; does not replace permissions). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/omo-jev-plugin.md).
- [omp-jev-compaction](https://github.com/jerryfane/omp-jev-compaction) - omp plugin: verbatim TypeSafe/OpenRouter Jev-scored tool-context reduction with sticky cache-friendly rewrites (distinct from fast-jev-compaction / jev-compact). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/omp-jev-compaction.md).
- [omp-jev-tier](https://github.com/charliemartin0/omp-jev-tier) - Extension for the omp coding agent that uses omp's native judge role (normally `typesafe/jev-latest`) for subagent model tiering and automatic plan mode, without patching omp or changing configured model roles, fallbacks or usage reserve; it refuses judgments from non-Jev judge models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/omp-jev-tier.md).
- [OneJev](https://github.com/OmniJev/OneJev) - Multimodal System One decision model (text/image/video → calibrated option probabilities in one forward pass); independent of official Jev; distinct from listed Jev-Omni and PlayJev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/onejev.md).
- [onesie](https://github.com/frodi-karlsson/onesie) - Unix-pipeable System One CLI for TypeSafe Jev (also OpenRouter/Berget): pipe text, ask a typed question, script the answer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/frodi-karlsson-onesie.md).
- [Open Alternative to Jev](https://github.com/ikermoel/open-alternative-jev) - Studies typed decisions from open models using packed or separate inference and optional calibration; independent of TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-alternative-jev.md).
- [open-source-finder](https://github.com/Tanishk901/open-source-finder) - Rank a GitHub repo's open issues for good-first-issue fit with TypeSafe Jev (size, clarity, project knowledge, claim flags). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-source-finder.md).
- [openai-decisions-vs-jev-vs-laya](https://github.com/mateusfonsek/openai-decisions-vs-jev-vs-laya) - Independent Portuguese-language benchmark of decision models — OpenAI Decisions API (`gpt-6-luna`), TypeSafe Jev (`jev-1.13.0`) and local Laya (with option rotation) — on agent tool routing, prompt-injection detection and LLM-as-judge in PT-BR: 450 cases per provider (2026-10-07), with accuracy, macro-F1, ECE, latency with and without network, and cost per 1k. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openai-decisions-vs-jev-vs-laya.md).
- [openclaw-jev (Hyper-AI-Lab)](https://github.com/Hyper-AI-Lab/openclaw-jev) - OpenClaw control plane: intake routing with TypeSafe Jev (≠ yousan openclaw-jev-* plugins). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hyper-ai-lab-openclaw-jev.md).
- [OpenCode Decisions plugin](https://github.com/matthewpetela/opencode-decisions-plugin) - OpenCode V2 plugin exposing a `decisions_evaluate` tool so an agent can ask typed questions of Jev (text, through OpenCode Console's Jev models) or OpenAI Decisions (text and images); the agent keeps the taxonomy and interprets the probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-decisions-plugin.md).
- [opencode-auto-effort](https://github.com/justmytwospence/opencode-auto-effort) - opencode plugin where TypeSafe Jev rates, in about 200 ms before each turn, how much careful reasoning a prompt needs (0–3) and sets opencode's model variant (low → xhigh) accordingly; cache-safe and sharing one settings file with the pi and Claude Code ports. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-auto-effort.md).
- [opencode-toolrouter](https://github.com/Nsilswal/opencode-toolrouter) - opencode plugin: TypeSafe Jev picks which MCP tools each request needs so schemas stay small. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-toolrouter.md).
- [OpenComputerUse](https://github.com/IAmJSD/OpenComputerUse) - Background computer use for agents as an MCP server: start a session with a desktop app, drive it by window coordinates or accessibility elements while your own pointer, focus and frontmost app stay put, and run `run_recipe` step lists where each step's interactive elements become one Jev `choice` question (or Cloudflare Clef-flash), acting only when the model is confident. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencomputeruse.md).
- [OpenDecider](https://github.com/manjunathshiva/opendecider) - Open System One decision models (choice/score/yes-no) with optional Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` serve—independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opendecider.md).
- [Open Jev Bridge](https://github.com/louis-szeto/open-jev-bridge) - Zero-dependency Node MCP + Claude Code/Codex hooks for hosted Jev or local Kev/Laya System One (compaction and completion gates). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-jev-bridge.md).
- [OpenJev (alanhuangyoo)](https://github.com/alanhuangyoo/OpenJev) - OpenJev (`wev-ai` / `openjev-ai` on PyPI): open, local System One decision models OpenJev-1.7B/4B/8B on Hugging Face that answer the `POST /v1/systemone` request shape for general decisions and browser-agent steps; independent, distinct from other projects named OpenJev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/alanhuangyoo-openjev.md).
- [OpenJev-Cactus](https://github.com/SiliconLabAI/OpenJev-Cactus) - High-perf CPU edge server with OpenAI chat completions plus System One `/v1/systemone` (Cactus/needle); distinct from other OpenJev listings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/siliconlabai-openjev-cactus.md).
- [OpenJev (ejhshen)](https://github.com/ejhshen/OpenJev) - OpenJev-4B open reproduction of Jev-style open-vocabulary probabilistic decisions (not TypeSafe-hosted; distinct from lookski/openjev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ejhshen-openjev.md).
- [openjev-rs](https://github.com/codesoda/openjev-rs) - Rust libraries for local typed decisions from frozen GGUF logits (OpenJev/SemIf readout); CLI/HTTP moved to codesoda/systemone. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codesoda-openjev-rs.md).
- [openjev-server (abhishekgahlot2)](https://github.com/abhishekgahlot2/openjev-server) - Decision API over open models (vLLM or MLX): one forward pass per question for choice/noul/score—the server behind OpenJev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/abhishekgahlot2-openjev-server.md).
- [Open Medical Jev](https://github.com/FeiLiuEM/open-medical-jev) - Jev-class medical judgments from frozen open models (no fine-tuning): dual-reader fusion, conformal routing, and published comparisons vs hosted Jev on licensing exams. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-medical-jev.md).
- [open-jev](https://github.com/nico-martin/open-jev) - Runs independent Kev and DeBERTa typed-decision models locally through Transformers.js, with browser WebGPU/WASM support; does not use official Jev weights. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-jev.md).
- [openclaw-jev-leakguard](https://github.com/yousan/openclaw-jev-leakguard) - OpenClaw outgoing-message guard: TypeSafe Jev (or local Kev) blocks client/credential leaks by destination tier. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-jev-leakguard.md).
- [openclaw-jev-trigger](https://github.com/yousan/openclaw-jev-trigger) - OpenClaw automations: plain-language triggers judged by TypeSafe Jev each tick so the model wakes only when it matters. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-jev-trigger.md).
- [openclaw-typesafe-ai](https://github.com/Olli0103/openclaw-typesafe-ai) - OpenClaw plugin registering optional `typesafe_decide` for explicit TypeSafe Jev decisions (SecretRef credentials; no hooks). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-typesafe-ai.md).
- [OpenCode Security Guard](https://github.com/koppert/opencode-security-guard) - Linux OpenCode shell permission guard: local check + TypeSafe Jev read-only score ≥0.90 for auto-allow. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-security-guard.md).
- [opencode-jev-compaction](https://github.com/radqnico/opencode-jev-compaction) - OpenCode compaction plugin that prunes stale tool calls/results with TypeSafe Jev instead of LLM summarization (port of tamaratran/fast-jev-compaction)—distinct from roshan-shaik-ml/fast-jev-opencode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/radqnico-opencode-jev-compaction.md).
- [opencode-jev-guard](https://github.com/CogFlux/opencode-jev-guard) - OpenCode 2 plugin: TypeSafe Jev triages every local/FarHand shell command before run (distinct from leepokai/jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-jev-guard.md).
- [opencode-jev-plugin (fsodanogm2dev)](https://github.com/fsodanogm2dev/opencode-jev-plugin) - OpenCode/OmO plugin: local Jev broker + System One hooks for safety gating, routing, and aggressive token saving. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fsodanogm2dev-opencode-jev-plugin.md).
- [opencode-jev-router](https://github.com/robertn702/opencode-jev-router) - OpenCode Responses proxy: TypeSafe Jev picks reasoning effort then pins Astra/Luna/Sol upstream (distinct from opencode-jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-jev-router.md).
- [opencode-smart-reasoning](https://github.com/d0nj/opencode-smart-reasoning) - OpenCode plugin: TypeSafe Jev (Zen SystemOne) picks per-request reasoning effort, fail-open without a key. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-smart-reasoning.md).
- [OpenJev](https://github.com/lookski/openjev) - Local Jev-compatible typed decisions via masked-logit softmax (not TypeSafe-hosted). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openjev.md).
- [openjev-mcp (markylaredo)](https://github.com/markylaredo/openjev-mcp) - MCP server exposing Jev via the public OpenJEV API (`jev_ask` / choice / score / noul); built for DeepSeek Harness, works in any MCP client. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/markylaredo-openjev-mcp.md).
- [openjev-sglang](https://github.com/ekzhang/openjev-sglang) - Implements a Jev-shaped HTTP decision API using Qwen and SGLang; independent model behavior and unspecified code licensing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openjev-sglang.md).
- [openjevx](https://github.com/muthuishere/openjevx) - Open-weight Jev-compatible System One server for jevx: local `/v1/systemone` (default :21118) from Laya fine-tune with RLCD; pairs with muthuishere/jevx. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/muthuishere-openjevx.md).
- [orca-jev-advisor](https://github.com/ab2webco/orca-jev-advisor) - Orca Lab decision layer: TypeSafe Jev judges risky agent commands while local rules cover obvious cases (license unspecified at tip). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/orca-jev-advisor.md).
- [Pad](https://github.com/PerpetualSoftware/pad) - Local-first project management (CLI, web UI, agent skill) for developers and coding agents; an optional TypeSafe decision provider asks Jev whether items need a human or are blocked and matches playbooks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pad.md).
- [painpoints](https://github.com/prodBirdy/painpoints) - CLI, MCP server and native viewer that score every source file on layering, complexity, data-access cost, failure handling, UI cost and trust-boundary risk with a System One model (TypeSafe Jev or Cloudflare Clef), then hand coding agents a ranked, self-explaining technical-debt report. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/painpoints.md).
- [paperclip-jev](https://github.com/tbmsindemnify/paperclip-jev) - Paperclip fork that routes work tasks automatically with TypeSafe Jev (see TYPesafe issue-router docs). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/paperclip-jev.md).
- [patdown](https://github.com/tyler-dot-earth/patdown) - Fuzzy markdown-rule linter CLI with a swappable judge; default backend posts typed questions to TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/patdown.md).
- [PDF Race](https://github.com/goodrahstar/pdf-race) - Timed Docling→TypeSafe Jev vs Gemini document race with committed keyless replays (optional local Laya on Apple silicon). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pdf-race.md).
- [PerfectRecall](https://github.com/arslanr-com/perfectrecall) - Hermes/Python agent memory using TypeSafe or OpenRouter Jev evidence questions over local SQLite (Mnemosyne-compatible; no embeddings). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/perfectrecall.md).
- [pg-jev](https://github.com/realZachi/pg-jev) - Adds semantic predicates, probabilities, choices, and scores to PostgreSQL through a PL/Python extension. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pg-jev.md).
- [pg_typesafe](https://github.com/giuliosmall/pg_typesafe) - Pre-alpha PostgreSQL C extension for TypeSafe Jev Choice/Noul/Score with batched multi-text helpers; distinct from pg-jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pg-typesafe.md).
- [Photoshop MCP](https://github.com/alisaitteke/photoshop-mcp) - Unofficial MCP server for Adobe Photoshop (100+ tools and one-step recipes); its built-in chat UI can opt in to TypeSafe Jev intent routing so confident, safe single commands run without an LLM call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/photoshop-mcp.md).
- [Pi Adaptive Effort Router (XDeviation)](https://github.com/XDeviation/pi-jev-router) - Pi extension that changes thinking level only via TypeSafe Jev (distinct from philippdubach/pi-jev-router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/xdeviation-pi-jev-router.md).
- [pi-advisor](https://github.com/philipbrembeck/pi-advisor) - Configurable Advisor/Executor Pi plugin with optional TypeSafe Jev consultation filter and proactive turn gate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-advisor.md).
- [pi-automode-classifier](https://github.com/deepu105/pi-automode-classifier) - Auto mode for the Pi coding agent: built-in rules decide most tool calls, then a classifier model — hosted Jev (e.g. `openrouter` `typesafe/jev-1.13` through Pi's model registry) or local Kev / Laya on CPU via llama.cpp's `/v1/systemone` — scores unknown shell commands, and risky ones get a confirm prompt or are blocked. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-automode-classifier.md).
- [pi-extensions (narumiruna)](https://github.com/narumiruna/pi-extensions) - Pi Coding Agent extension monorepo with TypeSafe packages for typed Jev decisions, compaction selection, and FTS5+Jev search. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/narumiruna-pi-extensions.md).
- [pi-follow-through](https://github.com/Nabsku/pi-follow-through) - Pi extension that asks TypeSafe Jev whether useful work remains after a run and nudges only with verifiable unfinished evidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-follow-through.md).
- [pi-heed](https://github.com/Nyarlathoteppppp/pi-heed) - Pi extension that keeps conversational user constraints as structured policy and checks side-effecting tool calls with TypeSafe Jev help. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-heed.md).
- [pi-jev](https://github.com/y0usaf/pi-jev) - Adds a TypeSafe Jev gate, output judge, and jev_ask tool to the Pi coding agent, with shadow mode by default and fail-open errors. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev.md).
- [pi-jev (kurowashi)](https://github.com/kurowashi/pi-jev) - Pi coding-agent extensions that use TypeSafe Jev for semantic checks on file edits and new-file placement. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kurowashi-pi-jev.md).
- [pi-Jev-browser](https://github.com/laihenyi/pi-Jev-browser) - Pi extension where TypeSafe Jev chooses each Playwright browser action over a structured DOM observation in a bounded loop. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-browser.md).
- [pi-jev-context](https://github.com/Nyarlathoteppppp/pi-jev-context) - Pi extension: cache-neutral write-time trimming of long tool outputs with TypeSafe Jev only when comparison cannot decide, plus lossless context_recall. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-context.md).
- [pi-jev-effort](https://github.com/namenu/pi-jev-effort) - Pi extension: TypeSafe Jev scores prompt difficulty and sets thinking level, capped by remaining quota/burn (distinct from pi-jev-router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-effort.md).
- [pi-jev-extension (rioliu)](https://github.com/rioliu/pi-jev-extension) - Pi agent extension adding a `jev_decide` tool for TypeSafe Jev typed choice/score/noul questions with session-model fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rioliu-pi-jev-extension.md).
- [pi-jev-permit](https://github.com/kurihada/pi-jev-permit) - Pi extension: TypeSafe Jev judges each bash/write/edit call after local hard-deny and read-only fast paths. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-permit.md).
- [pi-jev-router](https://github.com/philippdubach/pi-jev-router) - Pi extension: TypeSafe Jev classifies tasks and local policy routes OpenRouter models (Pareto profiles; shadow mode default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-router.md).
- [pi-jev-sentinel](https://github.com/harshwasan/pi-jev-sentinel) - Adds TypeSafe Jev checks for Pi, Claude Code, and Codex tool calls, tool outputs, and replies, with secret scrubbing and fail-closed asks when unconfigured. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-sentinel.md).
- [pi-next-prompt](https://github.com/justmytwospence/pi-next-prompt) - Pi extension with Claude Code–style next-prompt suggestions: the main model may end its final message with one `<next>…</next>` line, and TypeSafe Jev (through Pi's classifier models) vets whether it is a concrete, non-generic, not-yet-done next step; it is shown under the editor at p ≥ 0.6 and `Tab` fills it in without sending. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-next-prompt.md).
- [pi-reply-guard](https://github.com/amalavet/pi-reply-guard) - Pi extension that checks each agent reply against installed skills you choose: a classifier — Jev by default (`openrouter/typesafe/jev-1.13`) — returns the probability the reply adheres to each skill, and replies below your threshold are sent back for a rewrite (up to `maxRewrites`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-reply-guard.md).
- [pi-safely](https://github.com/dcowsill/pi-safely) - Pi package that re-implements the core of Claude Code's auto mode for the Pi agent, gating every non-allowlisted tool call through a System One decision model — Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter), Cloudflare Clef or any `POST /v1/systemone` backend — that returns calibrated yes/no probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-safely.md).
- [pi-shift-router](https://github.com/green-dalii/pi-shift-router) - Pi per-turn cheap/strong model router with failover and optional TypeSafe Jev decision-model judge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-shift-router.md).
- [pi-subagent-jev](https://github.com/G0-0000/pi-subagent-jev) - pi package: gate subagent dispatches through a Jev System One endpoint and expose jev_ask / jev_models tools (requires pi-subagents). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-subagent-jev.md).
- [pi-thinking-router-jev](https://github.com/Wh0rigin/pi-thinking-router-jev) - Pi coding-agent extension: TypeSafe Jev adapts thinking level (low→xhigh) from task start/failures with local-rule fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-thinking-router-jev.md).
- [pi-typesafe-approve](https://github.com/Davidasx/pi-typesafe-approve) - Pi extension that auto-approves routine Bash commands via a System One/Jev decision model and escalates the rest to a human. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-typesafe-approve.md).
- [pi-typesafe-bash-guard](https://github.com/gowthamgts/pi-stuff/tree/main/extensions/typesafe-bash-guard) - Pi extension that classifies bash tool calls and `!` shells with TypeSafe Jev before execution; supports 1Password `op://` API-key references. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-typesafe-bash-guard.md).
- [pi-verdict](https://github.com/jesset/pi-verdict) - Minimal Pi permission gate (allow/ask/deny) with an optional TypeSafe Jev decisions-model classifier adapter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-verdict.md).
- [pi-warden](https://github.com/DevMortimer/pi-warden) - Combines local Pi guard checks with Jev action and rule judgments, configurable holds, corrective feedback and context retention. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-warden.md).
- [pi-watcher](https://github.com/Maolon/pi-watcher) - Pi extension that watches long-running builds, tests, training jobs, CI and deadlines from durable evidence and keeps the session quiet until a fact needs a decision; optionally, TypeSafe Jev answers six bounded questions about a sanitized evidence window (progress, blocker, needs a decision, repeating, claim vs evidence, enough context) that can raise or annotate an episode but never override hard facts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-watcher.md).
- [pi-zereight-router](https://github.com/zereight/pi-zereight-router) - Jev-backed virtual model for Pi: each user message is classified by Jev (`openrouter/typesafe/jev-1.13`) as planning, research, codebase explore, review, implementation or other and routed to a fixed Cursor-provider model and effort, with a mid-turn handoff to the implementation model after the first file edit and a local phase decision log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-zereight-router.md).
- [plain](https://github.com/gabe4coding/plain) - Write browser, desktop and mobile end-to-end tests in plain English YAML: Playwright, xa11y or Appium perform the actions while Jev reads the accessibility tree to select the element each step describes and to judge each `expect` claim; coding agents can explore a flow via MCP and save it as a spec. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/plain.md).
- [plain-language-gate](https://github.com/Wujiaowang/plain-language-gate) - Agent skill/CLI: TypeSafe Jev runs six plain-language readability checks and returns pass/review/rewrite (author model rewrites). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/plain-language-gate.md).
- [PlayJev](https://github.com/OmniJev/PlayJev) - Plays ten browser games from the frame alone with an open 0.8B model that scores the moves the game lists in one forward pass; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/playjev.md)
- [PlayJev (filed)](https://github.com/filedcom/playjev) - "Stagehand, but with Jev": natural-language Playwright automation where TypeSafe Jev selects real browser nodes (`check`, `choose`, `rate`, `act`) and deterministic code performs actions; distinct from the listed OmniJev/PlayJev game player. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/filedcom-playjev.md).
- [playwright-jev](https://github.com/criguex/playwright-jev) - Semantic Playwright Test assertions (`toMean`, `toMeanAll`, `toChoose`, `toBeScored`) powered by TypeSafe Jev with fail-closed ambiguity handling and record/replay. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/criguex-playwright-jev.md).
- [playwright-jev (AdriaanVE)](https://github.com/AdriaanVE/playwright-jev) - Structured TypeSafe Jev decisions for Playwright failure triage, smart retries, healer gating, snapshot pruning, locator healing, and test impact ordering. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adriaanve-playwright-jev.md).
- [Poltergeist](https://github.com/ghostsecurity/poltergeist) - Ghost Security's fast Go secret scanner with an opt-in advisory classification that asks Jev how likely each detected value is a real secret, without changing findings or exit codes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/poltergeist.md).
- [polyjev](https://github.com/PraveenAShukla/polyjev) - Typed calibrated decisions (Noul/Choice/Score/Span) from any LLM with a drop-in Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` server; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/polyjev.md).
- [postgres-search](https://github.com/alexforman1/postgres-search) - Algolia-style product search in plain PostgreSQL (whole and partial words, typos, barcodes, typeahead, facets) with an optional TypeSafe Jev second stage asking two questions per search — keep or sink each result, and which spelling the user meant; the README doubles as a dated evaluation report on 440k USDA products with failures, costs and threats to validity. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/postgres-search.md).
- [Privatemode Decisions benchmark](https://github.com/edgelesssys/privatemode-decisions-benchmark) - Edgeless Systems' benchmark comparing its Privatemode Decisions library (GLM-5.3-Flash in an attested enclave) with TypeSafe Jev and local Laya on speed, accuracy, calibration and cost across 29 labelled public datasets, plus JevBench comparisons. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/privatemode-decisions-benchmark.md).
- [Prompt Rejector](https://github.com/revsmoke/promptrejectormcp) - MCP/HTTPS screening for prompts, skills, and tool descriptions with TypeSafe Jev plus deterministic checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prompt-rejector.md).
- [prompt2jev](https://github.com/sumleo/prompt2jev) - Agent skill and CLI that convert natural language, an LLM prompt, or prompt-running code into a TypeSafe Jev decision (typed questions + runnable script). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prompt2jev.md).
- [protoc-gen-jev](https://github.com/bufbuild/protoc-gen-jev) - Experimental Buf (bufbuild) protoc plugin that turns Protobuf schemas annotated with questions, rubrics and thresholds into strongly typed Jev clients for Go, TypeScript and Python (or a language-agnostic `.jev.json` spec), mapping fields to noul, choice and score decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/protoc-gen-jev.md).
